<script>
    (() => {

        "use strict";

        /* =========================================================
           ANDROID CONTRACTS
        ========================================================= */

        const bridge = window.AndroidInterface;

        const CURRENT_CONTRACTS = Object.freeze({

            list:
                "getShiftRecordsTyped",

            save:
                "saveShiftRecordTyped",

            update:
                "updateShiftRecord",

            remove:
                "deleteShiftRecord",

            report:
                "generateShiftReport"

        });


        /*
         * Contract جديد للبيانات المرجعية.
         *
         * يجب أن يعيده Android كـ JSON يحتوي مثلاً على:
         *
         * {
         *   "station": { "id": 1, "name": "..." },
         *   "branch": { "id": 1, "name": "..." },
         *   "current_user": { "id": 10, "branch_id": 1, "station_id": 1, ... },
         *   "cashiers": [ { "id": 20, "full_name_ar": "..." } ],
         *   "attendants": [ { "id": 30, "full_name_ar": "..." } ],
         *   "managers": [ { "id": 40, "full_name_ar": "...", "job_title": "مدير المحطة" } ]
         * }
         *
         * سيتم تصفية المديرين بناءً على job_title في القائمة التالية:
         *   "مدير المحطة", "نائب مدير المحطة", "مشرف وردية", "مساعد مدير العمليات"
         *
         * إذا لم يتم توفير job_title، سيتم اعتبار جميع المدراء مؤهلين.
         */

        const OPTIONS_CONTRACT =
            "getShiftFormContext";

        // المسميات الوظيفية المؤهلة للمدير
        const MANAGER_JOB_TITLES = [
            "مدير المحطة",
            "نائب مدير المحطة",
            "مشرف وردية",
            "مساعد مدير العمليات"
        ];


        /* =========================================================
           STATE
        ========================================================= */

        const state = {

            rows: [],

            filtered: [],

            filter: "all",

            editing: null,

            loadedAt: null,

            selectedAttendants: [],

            options: {

                stations: [],

                cashiers: [],

                attendants: [],

                managers: [],          // جميع المديرين من العقد

                qualifiedManagers: [], // المرشحون حسب job_title

                currentUser: null,

                currentManager: null,  // المدير المحدد مسبقًا

                branch: null,

                station: null

            },

            optionsLoaded: false

        };


        /* =========================================================
           HELPERS
        ========================================================= */

        const $ =
            id =>
                document.getElementById(id);


        function esc(value){

            return String(value ?? "—")
                .replace(
                    /[&<>"']/g,
                    c => ({
                        "&":"&amp;",
                        "<":"&lt;",
                        ">":"&gt;",
                        '"':"&quot;",
                        "'":"&#39;"
                    }[c])
                );

        }


        function number(value){

            const n =
                Number(value);

            return Number.isFinite(n)
                ? n
                : 0;

        }


        function today(){

            const d =
                new Date();

            const year =
                d.getFullYear();

            const month =
                String(
                    d.getMonth()+1
                ).padStart(2,"0");

            const day =
                String(
                    d.getDate()
                ).padStart(2,"0");

            return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;

        }


        function nowLocal(){

            const d =
                new Date();

            const year =
                d.getFullYear();

            const month =
                String(
                    d.getMonth()+1
                ).padStart(2,"0");

            const day =
                String(
                    d.getDate()
                ).padStart(2,"0");

            const hours =
                String(
                    d.getHours()
                ).padStart(2,"0");

            const minutes =
                String(
                    d.getMinutes()
                ).padStart(2,"0");

            return `${year}-${month}-${day}T${hours}:${minutes}`;

        }


        function toast(
            message,
            type="success"
        ){

            const node =
                $("toast");

            node.textContent =
                message;

            node.className =
                `toast show ${type}`;

            clearTimeout(
                node._timer
            );

            node._timer =
                setTimeout(
                    () =>
                        node.classList.remove(
                            "show"
                        ),
                    3600
                );

        }


        function loading(
            visible,
            message="جارٍ تنفيذ العملية..."
        ){

            $("loadingText")
                .textContent =
                message;

            $("loading")
                .classList
                .toggle(
                    "show",
                    Boolean(visible)
                );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           BRIDGE
        ========================================================= */

        function hasContract(
            method
        ){

            return Boolean(
                bridge &&
                typeof bridge[method] === "function"
            );

        }


        async function invoke(
            method,
            args=[]
        ){

            if(!hasContract(method)){

                throw new Error(
                    `عقد Android غير متاح: ${method}`
                );

            }

            let result;

            try{

                result =
                    bridge[method](...args);

            }catch(error){

                throw error;

            }


            if(
                result &&
                typeof result.then === "function"
            ){

                result =
                    await result;

            }

            return result;

        }


        function parseBridge(
            result
        ){

            if(
                result === null ||
                result === undefined ||
                result === ""
            ){

                return null;

            }

            if(
                typeof result === "object"
            ){

                return result;

            }

            try{

                return JSON.parse(result);

            }catch(_){

                return {
                    success:false,
                    error:"استجابة Bridge غير صالحة"
                };

            }

        }


        function requireSuccess(result){
            const parsed = parseBridge(result);
            if(!parsed || parsed.success !== true){
                throw new Error(parsed?.error || parsed?.message || "فشلت العملية ولم يؤكدها Bridge.");
            }
            return parsed;
        }

        function rowsFrom(
            result
        ){

            const parsed =
                parseBridge(result);

            if(Array.isArray(parsed)){

                return parsed;

            }

            if(
                Array.isArray(
                    parsed?.rows
                )
            ){

                return parsed.rows;

            }

            if(
                Array.isArray(
                    parsed?.data
                )
            ){

                return parsed.data;

            }

            if(
                Array.isArray(
                    parsed?.items
                )
            ){

                return parsed.items;

            }

            return [];

        }


        /* =========================================================
           ID
        ========================================================= */

        function idOf(row){

            return (
                row?.id ??
                row?.shift_id ??
                row?.shiftId ??
                ""
            );

        }


        function findRow(id){

            return state.rows.find(
                row =>
                    String(
                        idOf(row)
                    ) === String(id)
            );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           SHIFT CODE (توليد تلقائي مخفي)
        ========================================================= */

        function generateShiftCode(){

            let max = 0;

            state.rows.forEach(
                row => {

                    const code =
                        String(
                            row?.shift_code ?? ""
                        );

                    const match =
                        code.match(
                            /(\d+)$/
                        );

                    if(match){

                        max =
                            Math.max(
                                max,
                                Number(match[1])
                            );

                    }

                }
            );

            return (
                "SH-" +
                String(
                    max + 1
                ).padStart(
                    6,
                    "0"
                )
            );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           SHIFT TYPE
        ========================================================= */

        function translateShiftType(
            value
        ){

            const map = {

                morning:
                    "صباحية",

                evening:
                    "مسائية",

                night:
                    "ليلية",

                full_day:
                    "يوم كامل",

                صباحية:
                    "صباحية",

                مسائية:
                    "مسائية",

                ليلية:
                    "ليلية",

                "يوم كامل":
                    "يوم كامل"

            };

            return (
                map[value] ||
                value ||
                "—"
            );

        }


        function detectShiftType(){

            const hour =
                new Date()
                    .getHours();

            if(
                hour >= 6 &&
                hour < 14
            ){

                return "morning";

            }

            if(
                hour >= 14 &&
                hour < 22
            ){

                return "evening";

            }

            return "night";

        }


        /* =========================================================
           OPTIONS (تحميل البيانات المرجعية)
        ========================================================= */

        // Keep the active control visible above the sticky footer when Android opens the keyboard.
        document.addEventListener('focusin', event => {
            const target = event.target;
            if (!target || !target.matches || !target.matches('#shiftForm input, #shiftForm select, #shiftForm textarea')) return;
            window.setTimeout(() => target.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest', inline:'nearest'}), 120);
        });

        function wrapShiftFormRows(rows){
            const form = $('shiftForm');
            if(!form) return;
            rows.forEach(ids => {
                const fields = ids.map(id => document.getElementById(id)?.closest('.form-group')).filter(Boolean);
                if(!fields.length) return;
                /* Skip wrapping single full-width fields — keep them as direct children of #shiftForm */
                if(fields.length === 1 && fields[0].classList.contains('full')) return;
                const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                wrapper.className = 'form-row' + (ids.length === 3 ? ' shift-row-triple' : '');
                form.insertBefore(wrapper, fields[0]);
                fields.forEach(field => wrapper.appendChild(field));
            });
        }
        function applyShiftMobileFormOrder(){
            // ترتيب الحقول في سطور: كل اثنين معاً، والحقول المطلوب عرضها كاملة في سطر واحد
            const rows = [
                ['shift_type','shift_date'],
                ['start_time','end_time'],
                ['duration_minutes'],            // عرض كامل
                ['manager_id','cashier_id'],
                ['attendant_ids'],               // عرض كامل
                ['opening_cash','closing_cash'],
                ['opening_credit','closing_credit'],
                ['opening_bank','closing_bank'],
                ['total_fuel_sales','total_product_sales','total_service_sales'],
                ['total_sales','total_discounts'],
                ['total_tax','total_vat','total_fuel_liters'],
                ['total_cash','total_credit_card'],
                ['total_bank_transfer','total_credit_sales'],
                ['total_cheque','total_other'],
                ['cash_variance'],               // عرض كامل
                ['variance_reason']              // عرض كامل
            ];
            wrapShiftFormRows(rows);
            rows.forEach((ids, rowIndex) => ids.forEach((id, columnIndex) => {
                const field = document.getElementById(id);
                const group = field && field.closest('.form-group');
                if (!group) return;
                group.style.setProperty('--shift-order', String((rowIndex + 1) * 10 + columnIndex));
                if (ids.length === 3) group.classList.add('shift-form-triple');
            }));
            document.querySelectorAll('#shiftForm > .section-title').forEach(title => {
                const label = (title.textContent || '').trim();
                title.style.setProperty('--shift-order', label.includes('الأرصدة') ? '60' : label.includes('المبيعات') ? '100' : '140');
            });
        }

        applyShiftMobileFormOrder();

        async function loadFormOptions(force = false){

            if(state.optionsLoaded && !force){
                return;
            }

            if(!hasContract(OPTIONS_CONTRACT)){
                showOptionError("عقد بيانات نموذج الوردية غير متاح في Android.");
                return;
            }

            try{
                const result = await invoke(OPTIONS_CONTRACT);
                const data = parseBridge(result) || {};

                // قراءة البيانات
                const station = data.station || null;
                const branch = data.branch || null;
                const currentUser = data.current_user || data.currentUser || null;

                // المدراء (جميعهم)
                const allManagers = Array.isArray(data.managers) ? data.managers : [];

                // تصفية المدراء المؤهلين حسب job_title
                const qualifiedManagers = allManagers.filter(emp => {
                    const title = (emp.job_title || emp.jobTitle || '').trim();
                    if (!title) return true; // إذا لم يحدد job_title نعتبره مؤهل (حفظ التوافق)
                    return MANAGER_JOB_TITLES.some(t => title.includes(t));
                });

                state.options = {
                    stations: station ? [station] : [],
                    cashiers: Array.isArray(data.cashiers) ? data.cashiers : [],
                    attendants: Array.isArray(data.attendants) ? data.attendants : [],
                    managers: allManagers,
                    qualifiedManagers: qualifiedManagers,
                    currentUser: currentUser,
                    currentManager: null,
                    branch: branch,
                    station: station
                };

                // تعيين المدير الحالي إذا كان من ضمن المؤهلين
                if (currentUser && qualifiedManagers.some(m => String(m.id) === String(currentUser.id))) {
                    state.options.currentManager = qualifiedManagers.find(m => String(m.id) === String(currentUser.id));
                }

                state.optionsLoaded = true;

                // تعبئة الحقول المخفية station_id و branch_id من المستخدم الحالي
                const stationIdEl = $("station_id");
                const branchIdEl = $("branch_id");
                if (stationIdEl) {
                    stationIdEl.value = currentUser?.station_id ?? currentUser?.stationId ?? station?.id ?? '';
                }
                if (branchIdEl) {
                    branchIdEl.value = currentUser?.branch_id ?? currentUser?.branchId ?? branch?.id ?? '';
                }

                // تعبئة القوائم
                renderCashierOptions();
                renderManagerOptions();   // تستخدم qualifiedManagers
                renderAttendantOptions();

                // تعيين أمين الصندوق تلقائياً إذا كان هناك واحد فقط
                if (state.options.cashiers.length === 1) {
                    const cashier = state.options.cashiers[0];
                    const cashierId = cashier.id ?? cashier.employee_id ?? "";
                    if (cashierId) {
                        $("cashier_id").value = String(cashierId);
                    }
                }

                // تعيين المدير تلقائياً إذا كان هناك واحد مؤهل فقط
                if (state.options.qualifiedManagers.length === 1) {
                    const manager = state.options.qualifiedManagers[0];
                    const managerId = manager.id || manager.employee_id || "";
                    if (managerId) {
                        $("manager_id").value = String(managerId);
                    }
                } else if (state.options.currentManager) {
                    // إذا كان المستخدم الحالي مديراً مؤهلاً
                    const managerId = state.options.currentManager.id || state.options.currentManager.employee_id || "";
                    if (managerId) {
                        $("manager_id").value = String(managerId);
                    }
                }

            }catch(error){
                state.optionsLoaded = false;
                showOptionError(error.message);
            }
        }

        function showOptionError(
            message
        ){

            const text =
                message ||
                "تعذر تحميل البيانات المرجعية من قاعدة البيانات.";

            toast(
                text,
                "warning"
            );

        }


        function renderCashierOptions(){

            const select =
                $("cashier_id");

            select.innerHTML =
                `<option value="">
                    اختر أمين الصندوق
                 </option>`;

            state.options.cashiers
                .forEach(
                    person => {

                        const option =
                            document.createElement(
                                "option"
                            );

                        // The typed contract stores employees.id in cashier_employee_id.
                        // A cashier never needs a linked users row to be selectable.
                        option.value = person.id ?? person.employee_id ?? "";
                        option.dataset.employeeId = person.id ?? person.employee_id ?? "";
                        option.dataset.persistedId = person.employee_id ?? option.value;

                        option.textContent =
                            person.full_name_ar ??
                            person.full_name ??
                            person.name ??
                            person.employee_name ??
                            `موظف ${option.dataset.employeeId || option.value}`;

                        select.appendChild(
                            option
                        );

                    }
                );

        }


        function renderManagerOptions(){

            const select =
                $("manager_id");

            if(!select){
                return;
            }

            select.innerHTML =
                `<option value="">اختر مدير الوردية</option>`;

            const managers =
                state.options.qualifiedManagers || [];

            if (managers.length === 0) {
                select.innerHTML = `<option value="">لا يوجد مدير وردية مؤهل</option>`;
                return;
            }

            managers.forEach(
                manager => {

                    const id =
                        manager.id ??
                        manager.employee_id ??
                        "";

                    if(!id){
                        return;
                    }

                    const name =
                        manager.full_name_ar ??
                        manager.full_name ??
                        manager.name ??
                        manager.employee_name ??
                        "موظف";

                    const option =
                        document.createElement("option");

                    option.value = id;
                    option.textContent = name;

                    // تعيين التحديد إذا كان هذا المدير هو المستخدم الحالي أو المدير المختار
                    const currentId =
                        state.options.currentManager?.id ??
                        state.options.currentManager?.employee_id;

                    if(currentId != null && String(currentId) === String(id)){
                        option.selected = true;
                    }

                    select.appendChild(option);
                }
            );

        }


        function renderAttendantOptions(){

            const select =
                $("attendantPicker");

            select.innerHTML =
                `<option value="">
                    اختر العامل لإضافته
                 </option>`;

            state.options.attendants
                .forEach(
                    person => {

                        const id =
                            person.id ??
                            person.employee_id;

                        if(
                            state.selectedAttendants
                                .some(
                                    x =>
                                        String(x.id) ===
                                        String(id)
                                )
                        ){

                            return;

                        }

                        const option =
                            document.createElement(
                                "option"
                            );

                        option.value =
                            id;

                        option.textContent =
                            person.full_name_ar ??
                            person.full_name ??
                            person.name ??
                            person.employee_name ??
                            `عامل ${id}`;

                        select.appendChild(
                            option
                        );

                    }
                );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           ATTENDANTS
        ========================================================= */

        $("attendantPicker")
            .addEventListener(
                "change",
                event => {

                    const id =
                        event.target.value;

                    if(!id){

                        return;

                    }

                    const person =
                        state.options.attendants
                            .find(
                                x =>
                                    String(
                                        x.id ??
                                        x.employee_id
                                    ) ===
                                    String(id)
                            );

                    if(!person){

                        return;

                    }

                    state.selectedAttendants
                        .push({

                            id:
                                person.id ??
                                person.employee_id,

                            name:
                                person.full_name_ar ??
                                person.full_name ??
                                person.name ??
                                person.employee_name ??
                                `عامل ${id}`

                        });

                    syncAttendants();

                    renderAttendantOptions();

                    event.target.value =
                        "";

                }
            );


        function syncAttendants(){

            $("attendant_ids")
                .value =
                state.selectedAttendants
                    .map(
                        x =>
                            x.id
                    )
                    .join(",");

            const box =
                $("attendantsBox");

            box.querySelectorAll(
                ".selected-person"
            )
                .forEach(
                    node =>
                        node.remove()
                );

            state.selectedAttendants
                .forEach(
                    person => {

                        const chip =
                            document.createElement(
                                "span"
                            );

                        chip.className =
                            "selected-person";

                        chip.innerHTML =
                            `${esc(person.name)}
                             <button
                                type="button"
                                aria-label="إزالة"
                                style="
                                    border:0;
                                    background:none;
                                    color:inherit;
                                    padding:0;
                                "
                             >
                                ×
                             </button>`;

                        chip.querySelector(
                            "button"
                        ).onclick =
                            () => {

                                state.selectedAttendants =
                                    state.selectedAttendants
                                        .filter(
                                            x =>
                                                String(x.id) !==
                                                String(person.id)
                                        );

                                syncAttendants();

                                renderAttendantOptions();

                            };

                        box.insertBefore(
                            chip,
                            $("attendantPicker")
                        );

                    }
                );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           FORM DEFAULTS
        ========================================================= */

        function resetForm(){

            $("shiftForm")
                .reset();

            state.selectedAttendants =
                [];

            // توليد رمز الوردية (مخفي)
            $("shift_code")
                .value =
                generateShiftCode();

            $("shift_date")
                .value =
                today();

            $("start_time")
                .value =
                nowLocal();

            $("shift_type")
                .value =
                detectShiftType();

            // تعبئة station_id و branch_id من currentUser (تظل مخفية)
            const user = state.options.currentUser;
            if (user) {
                const stationId = user.station_id ?? user.stationId ?? state.options.station?.id ?? '';
                const branchId = user.branch_id ?? user.branchId ?? state.options.branch?.id ?? '';
                $("station_id").value = String(stationId);
                $("branch_id").value = String(branchId);
            }

            $("duration_minutes")
                .value =
                "";

            $("cash_variance")
                .value =
                "";

            $("validationBox")
                .classList
                .remove("show");

            syncAttendants();

            renderAttendantOptions();

            // إعادة تعيين قائمة المديرين
            renderManagerOptions();

            // إذا كان هناك مدير تلقائي، حدده
            if (state.options.qualifiedManagers.length === 1) {
                const mgr = state.options.qualifiedManagers[0];
                const id = mgr.id || mgr.employee_id || "";
                if (id) $("manager_id").value = String(id);
            } else if (state.options.currentManager) {
                const id = state.options.currentManager.id || state.options.currentManager.employee_id || "";
                if (id) $("manager_id").value = String(id);
            }

        }


        function openForm(
            row=null
        ){

            state.editing =
                row;

            resetForm();

            $("formTitle")
                .textContent =
                row
                    ? "تعديل بيانات الوردية"
                    : "إضافة وردية جديدة";


            // محاولة تحميل الخيارات إذا لم تكن محملة
            if(!state.optionsLoaded){
                loadFormOptions(true);
            }


            if(row){

                Object.keys(row)
                    .forEach(
                        key => {

                            const element =
                                $(key);

                            if(!element){

                                return;

                            }

                            element.value =
                                row[key] ??
                                "";

                        }
                    );


                state.selectedAttendants =
                    parseAttendantIds(
                        row.attendant_ids
                    );

                syncAttendants();

                $("shift_code")
                    .readOnly =
                    true;

                // تعيين station_id و branch_id من row إذا وجدت
                if (row.station_id) $("station_id").value = row.station_id;
                if (row.branch_id) $("branch_id").value = row.branch_id;

                // تعيين المدير المختار من row
                if (row.manager_id) {
                    $("manager_id").value = String(row.manager_id);
                }

            }


            $("formOverlay")
                .classList
                .add("show");

        }


        function parseAttendantIds(
            value
        ){

            if(
                Array.isArray(value)
            ){

                return value.map(
                    id => ({
                        id,
                        name:
                            resolveEmployeeName(
                                id
                            )
                    })
                );

            }

            if(
                typeof value === "string" &&
                value.trim()
            ){

                return value
                    .split(",")
                    .map(
                        x =>
                            x.trim()
                    )
                    .filter(Boolean)
                    .map(
                        id => ({
                            id,
                            name:
                                resolveEmployeeName(
                                    id
                                )
                        })
                    );

            }

            return [];

        }


        function resolveEmployeeName(
            id
        ){

            const person =
                state.options.attendants
                    .find(
                        x =>
                            String(
                                x.id ??
                                x.employee_id
                            ) ===
                            String(id)
                    );

            return (
                person?.full_name_ar ??
                person?.full_name ??
                person?.display_name ??
                person?.username ??
                "—"
            );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           AUTOMATIC CALCULATIONS
        ========================================================= */

        function calculateDuration(){

            const start =
                $("start_time").value;

            const end =
                $("end_time").value;

            if(
                !start ||
                !end
            ){

                $("duration_minutes")
                    .value =
                    "";

                return;

            }

            const startDate =
                new Date(start);

            const endDate =
                new Date(end);

            const diff =
                Math.round(
                    (
                        endDate -
                        startDate
                    ) / 60000
                );

            $("duration_minutes")
                .value =
                diff >= 0
                    ? diff
                    : "";

        }


        function calculateVariance(){

            const opening =
                number(
                    $("opening_cash").value
                );

            const closing =
                number(
                    $("closing_cash").value
                );

            const cashSales =
                number(
                    $("total_cash").value
                );

            if(
                !(
                    $("closing_cash").value ||
                    $("opening_cash").value ||
                    $("total_cash").value
                )
            ){

                $("cash_variance")
                    .value =
                    "";

                return;

            }

            const variance =
                closing -
                (
                    opening +
                    cashSales
                );

            $("cash_variance")
                .value =
                variance.toFixed(2);

        }


        $("end_time")
            .addEventListener(
                "change",
                calculateDuration
            );

        $("start_time")
            .addEventListener(
                "change",
                calculateDuration
            );


        [
            "opening_cash",
            "closing_cash",
            "total_cash"
        ]
            .forEach(
                id => {

                    $(id)
                        .addEventListener(
                            "input",
                            calculateVariance
                        );

                }
            );


        /* =========================================================
           VALIDATION
        ========================================================= */

        function validateForm(){

            const errors =
                [];

            // station_id و branch_id يتم تعيينهما تلقائياً ولا نحتاج لتحقق

            if(
                !$("shift_date").value
            ){

                errors.push(
                    "تاريخ الوردية مطلوب."
                );

            }

            if(
                !$("shift_type").value
            ){

                errors.push(
                    "نوع الوردية مطلوب."
                );

            }

            if(
                !$("start_time").value
            ){

                errors.push(
                    "وقت بداية الوردية مطلوب."
                );

            }

            const end =
                $("end_time").value;

            const start =
                $("start_time").value;

            if(
                start &&
                end &&
                new Date(end) <
                new Date(start)
            ){

                errors.push(
                    "وقت نهاية الوردية لا يمكن أن يسبق وقت البداية."
                );

            }

            const cashier =
                $("cashier_id").value;

            if(
                !cashier
            ){

                errors.push(
                    "يجب تحديد أمين الصندوق عند إنشاء الوردية."
                );

            }

            // لا نطلب مديرًا إلزامياً، لكن نتحقق من صحة قيمته إذا اختاره المستخدم
            const manager = $("manager_id").value;
            if (manager && !state.options.qualifiedManagers.some(m => String(m.id || m.employee_id) === String(manager))) {
                errors.push("المدير المحدد غير مؤهل (يجب أن يكون مدير محطة أو نائب مدير أو مشرف وردية).");
            }

            const variance =
                number(
                    $("cash_variance").value
                );

            if(
                Math.abs(variance) > 0.009 &&
                !$("variance_reason").value.trim()
            ){

                errors.push(
                    "يوجد انحراف نقدي؛ يجب إدخال سبب الانحراف."
                );

            }

            showValidation(
                errors
            );

            return errors.length === 0;

        }


        function showValidation(
            errors
        ){

            const box =
                $("validationBox");

            if(
                !errors.length
            ){

                box.innerHTML =
                    "";

                box.classList
                    .remove(
                        "show"
                    );

                return;

            }

            box.innerHTML =
                `
                <strong>
                    يرجى معالجة الملاحظات التالية:
                </strong>

                <ul>
                    ${errors
                        .map(
                            error =>
                                `<li>${esc(error)}</li>`
                        )
                        .join("")}
                </ul>
                `;

            box.classList
                .add(
                    "show"
                );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           COLLECT FORM
        ========================================================= */

        function collectForm(){

            const payload =
                {};

            // نجمع كل حقول النموذج ذات المعرف
            $("shiftForm")
                .querySelectorAll(
                    "[id]"
                )
                .forEach(
                    element => {

                        const key =
                            element.id;

                        // نستثني الحقول المخفية التي نريد التعامل معها بشكل خاص (station_id, branch_id)
                        if (key === "shift_code") return; // shift_code نرسله بشكل منفصل
                        if (key === "station_id" || key === "branch_id") return; // نتعامل معها يدوياً

                        let value =
                            element.value;

                        if(
                            element.type ===
                            "number"
                        ){

                            value =
                                value === ""
                                    ? 0
                                    : Number(value);

                        }

                        payload[key] =
                            value;

                    }
                );


            payload.shift_code =
                state.editing?.shift_code ||
                $("shift_code").value;

            // إضافة station_id و branch_id من الحقول المخفية
            payload.station_id = Number($("station_id").value || 0);
            payload.branch_id = Number($("branch_id").value || 0);

            // attendant_ids
            payload.attendant_ids =
                $("attendant_ids").value;

            // cashier_id و manager_id كأرقام
            payload.cashier_id =
                $("cashier_id").value
                    ? Number($("cashier_id").value)
                    : null;

            payload.manager_id =
                $("manager_id").value
                    ? Number($("manager_id").value)
                    : null;

            // إزالة shift_code من payload لأنه يُرسل بشكل منفصل في saveShiftRecordTyped
            // لكننا أضفناه أعلاه، سنحذفه بعد ذلك
            // لكننا نستخدمه في payload للرجوع إليه في التحديث
            // سنبقي shift_code في payload للاستخدام العام

            return payload;

        }


        /* =========================================================
           SAVE
        ========================================================= */

        async function saveForm(){

            // التأكد من تحميل الخيارات قبل التحقق
            if(!state.optionsLoaded){
                await loadFormOptions(true);
            }

            if(
                !validateForm()
            ){

                toast(
                    "يرجى معالجة أخطاء الإدخال أولاً.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;

            }


            const payload =
                collectForm();


            loading(
                true,
                state.editing
                    ? "جارٍ تحديث الوردية..."
                    : "جارٍ حفظ الوردية..."
            );


            try{

                let result;


                if(
                    state.editing
                ){

                    const id =
                        idOf(
                            state.editing
                        );

                    if(!id){

                        throw new Error(
                            "معرف الوردية غير صالح."
                        );

                    }

                    result =
                        await invoke(
                            CURRENT_CONTRACTS.update,
                            [
                                Number(id),
                                JSON.stringify(
                                    payload
                                )
                            ]
                        );

                }else{

                    // استخدام saveShiftRecordTyped مع المعاملات المطلوبة
                    const stationId = Number(payload.station_id || 0);
                    const branchId = Number(payload.branch_id || 0);
                    const shiftType = String(payload.shift_type || detectShiftType());
                    const startTime = String(payload.start_time || nowLocal());
                    const managerId = Number(payload.manager_id || 0);
                    const cashierId = Number(payload.cashier_id || 0);
                    const attendantIds = String(payload.attendant_ids || "");
                    const openingCash = Number(payload.opening_cash || 0);
                    const openingBank = Number(payload.opening_bank || 0);
                    const openingCredit = Number(payload.opening_credit || 0);
                    const remarks = String(payload.variance_reason || "");

                    result =
                        await invoke(
                            CURRENT_CONTRACTS.save,
                            [
                                stationId,
                                branchId,
                                shiftType,
                                startTime,
                                managerId,
                                cashierId,
                                attendantIds,
                                openingCash,
                                openingBank,
                                openingCredit,
                                remarks
                            ]
                        );

                }


                requireSuccess(result);

                toast(
                    state.editing
                        ? "تم تحديث الوردية بنجاح."
                        : "تم حفظ الوردية في قاعدة البيانات.",
                    "success"
                );


                closeModal(
                    "formOverlay"
                );


                await reloadData();


            }catch(error){

                toast(
                    error.message ||
                    "فشلت عملية حفظ الوردية.",
                    "error"
                );

            }finally{

                loading(
                    false
                );

            }

        }


        /* =========================================================
           FILTERS
        ========================================================= */

        function setFilter(
            filter,
            button
        ){

            state.filter =
                filter;


            document
                .querySelectorAll(
                    ".tab,.nav-item"
                )
                .forEach(
                    element =>
                        element.classList
                            .remove(
                                "active"
                            )
                );


            if(button){

                button.classList
                    .add(
                        "active"
                    );

            }


            document
                .querySelectorAll(
                    `.tab[data-filter="${filter}"]`
                )
                .forEach(
                    element =>
                        element.classList
                            .add(
                                "active"
                            )
                );


            applyFilter();

        }


        document
            .querySelectorAll(
                ".tab"
            )
            .forEach(
                button => {

                    button.addEventListener(
                        "click",
                        () =>
                            setFilter(
                                button.dataset.filter,
                                button
                            )
                    );

                }
            );


        function applyFilter(){

            const query =
                (
                    $("searchInput")
                        .value ||
                    ""
                )
                    .trim()
                    .toLowerCase();


            let rows =
                [
                    ...state.rows
                ];


            if(
                state.filter ===
                "open"
            ){

                rows =
                    rows.filter(
                        row =>
                            !row?.end_time
                    );

            }


            if(
                state.filter ===
                "closed"
            ){

                rows =
                    rows.filter(
                        row =>
                            Boolean(
                                row?.end_time
                            )
                    );

            }


            if(
                state.filter ===
                "today"
            ){

                rows =
                    rows.filter(
                        row =>
                            String(
                                row?.shift_date ??
                                ""
                            )
                                .slice(0,10) ===
                            today()
                    );

            }


            if(
                state.filter ===
                "high"
            ){

                rows.sort(
                    (a,b) =>
                        number(
                            b?.total_sales
                        ) -
                        number(
                            a?.total_sales
                        )
                );

            }


            if(query){

                rows =
                    rows.filter(
                        row =>
                            JSON.stringify(
                                row
                            )
                                .toLowerCase()
                                .includes(
                                    query
                                )
                    );

            }


            state.filtered =
                rows;


            render();

        }


        /* =========================================================
           STATS
        ========================================================= */

        function renderStats(){

            const rows =
                state.filtered;


            $("statCount")
                .textContent =
                rows.length
                    .toLocaleString("ar");


            $("itemCount")
                .textContent =
                rows.length
                    .toLocaleString("ar");


            const open =
                rows.filter(
                    row =>
                        !row?.end_time
                ).length;


            $("statOpen")
                .textContent =
                open.toLocaleString("ar");


            const sales =
                rows.reduce(
                    (
                        sum,
                        row
                    ) =>
                        sum +
                        number(
                            row?.total_sales
                        ),
                    0
                );


            $("statSales")
                .textContent =
                sales.toLocaleString(
                    "ar",
                    {
                        maximumFractionDigits:2
                    }
                );


            const variance =
                rows.reduce(
                    (
                        sum,
                        row
                    ) =>
                        sum +
                        number(
                            row?.cash_variance
                        ),
                    0
                );


            $("statVariance")
                .textContent =
                variance.toLocaleString(
                    "ar",
                    {
                        maximumFractionDigits:2
                    }
                );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           RENDER
        ========================================================= */

        function render(){

            renderStats();


            const box =
                $("records");


            if(
                !state.filtered.length
            ){

                box.innerHTML =
                    `
                    <div class="empty">

                        <i class="fas fa-clock"></i>

                        <strong>
                            لا توجد ورديات مطابقة
                        </strong>

                        <small>
                            لا توجد سجلات أعادتها قاعدة البيانات ضمن الفلتر الحالي.
                        </small>

                    </div>
                    `;

                return;

            }


            box.innerHTML =
                state.filtered
                    .map(
                        (
                            row,
                            index
                        ) => {

                            const id =
                                esc(
                                    idOf(row) ||
                                    index
                                );

                            const code =
                                esc(
                                    row?.shift_code ||
                                    "بدون رمز"
                                );

                            const station =
                                resolveStationName(
                                    row?.station_id
                                );

                            const open =
                                !row?.end_time;

                            const sales =
                                number(
                                    row?.total_sales
                                );

                            const variance =
                                number(
                                    row?.cash_variance
                                );


                            return `
                    <article class="shift-card">

                        <div class="shift-head">

                            <div class="shift-main">

                                <div class="shift-code">
                                    ${code}
                                </div>

                                <div class="shift-meta">

                                    <span class="meta-pill">
                                        <i class="fas fa-gas-pump"></i>
                                        ${esc(station)}
                                    </span>

                                    <span class="meta-pill">
                                        <i class="fas fa-calendar"></i>
                                        ${esc(row?.shift_date)}
                                    </span>

                                    <span class="meta-pill">
                                        <i class="fas fa-clock"></i>
                                        ${esc(
                                            translateShiftType(
                                                row?.shift_type
                                            )
                                        )}
                                    </span>

                                </div>

                                <span
                                    class="status ${
                                        open
                                            ? "open"
                                            : "closed"
                                    }">

                                    <i class="fas ${
                                        open
                                            ? "fa-lock-open"
                                            : "fa-lock"
                                    }"></i>

                                    ${
                                        open
                                            ? "وردية مفتوحة"
                                            : "وردية مغلقة"
                                    }

                                </span>

                            </div>


                            <div class="shift-total">

                                <strong>
                                    ${
                                        sales.toLocaleString(
                                            "ar",
                                            {
                                                maximumFractionDigits:2
                                            }
                                        )
                                    }
                                </strong>

                                <span>
                                    إجمالي المبيعات
                                </span>

                            </div>

                        </div>


                        <div class="shift-grid">

                            ${info(
                                "البداية",
                                row?.start_time
                            )}

                            ${info(
                                "النهاية",
                                row?.end_time ||
                                "مفتوحة"
                            )}

                            ${info(
                                "المدة",
                                row?.duration_minutes != null
                                    ? `${row.duration_minutes} دقيقة`
                                    : "—"
                            )}

                            ${info(
                                "مدير الوردية",
                                row?.manager_name_ar || row?.manager_name || resolveManagerName(row?.manager_id)
                            )}

                            ${info(
                                "أمين الصندوق",
                                row?.cashier_name_ar || row?.cashier_name || resolveCashierName(row?.cashier_id)
                            )}

                            ${info(
                                "العاملون",
                                row?.attendant_names_ar || row?.attendant_names || "—"
                            )}

                            ${info(
                                "مبيعات الوقود",
                                money(
                                    row?.total_fuel_sales
                                )
                            )}

                            ${info(
                                "مبيعات المنتجات",
                                money(
                                    row?.total_product_sales
                                )
                            )}

                            ${info(
                                "الانحراف",
                                money(
                                    variance
                                )
                            )}

                        </div>


                        <div class="actions">

                            <button
                                class="btn"
                                data-view="${id}">

                                <i class="fas fa-eye"></i>

                                التفاصيل

                            </button>


                            <button
                                class="btn warning"
                                data-edit="${id}">

                                <i class="fas fa-pen"></i>

                                تعديل

                            </button>


                            ${
                                open
                                    ? `
                                    <button
                                        class="btn success"
                                        data-close="${id}">

                                        <i class="fas fa-lock"></i>

                                        إنهاء الوردية

                                    </button>
                                    `
                                    : ""
                            }


                            <button
                                class="btn danger"
                                data-delete="${id}">

                                <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>

                                حذف

                            </button>

                        </div>

                    </article>
                    `;

                        }
                    )
                    .join("");


            box.querySelectorAll(
                "[data-view]"
            )
                .forEach(
                    button => {

                        button.onclick =
                            () =>
                                openDetails(
                                    findRow(
                                        button.dataset.view
                                    )
                                );

                    }
                );


            box.querySelectorAll(
                "[data-edit]"
            )
                .forEach(
                    button => {

                        button.onclick =
                            () =>
                                openForm(
                                    findRow(
                                        button.dataset.edit
                                    )
                                );

                    }
                );


            box.querySelectorAll(
                "[data-delete]"
            )
                .forEach(
                    button => {

                        button.onclick =
                            () =>
                                deleteRow(
                                    findRow(
                                        button.dataset.delete
                                    )
                                );

                    }
                );


            box.querySelectorAll(
                "[data-close]"
            )
                .forEach(
                    button => {

                        button.onclick =
                            () =>
                                closeShift(
                                    findRow(
                                        button.dataset.close
                                    )
                                );

                    }
                );

        }


        function info(
            label,
            value
        ){

            return `
                <div class="info">

                    <label>
                        ${esc(label)}
                    </label>

                    <strong>
                        ${esc(value)}
                    </strong>

                </div>
            `;

        }


        function money(
            value
        ){

            return number(value)
                .toLocaleString(
                    "ar",
                    {
                        maximumFractionDigits:2
                    }
                );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           RESOLVERS
        ========================================================= */

        function resolveStationName(
            id
        ){

            const station =
                state.options.stations
                    .find(
                        x =>
                            String(
                                x.id ??
                                x.station_id
                            ) ===
                            String(id)
                    );

            return (
                station?.station_name_ar ??
                station?.station_name ??
                "—"
            );

        }


        function resolveCashierName(
            id
        ){

            const cashier =
                state.options.cashiers
                    .find(
                        x =>
                            String(
                                x.id ??
                                x.employee_id
                            ) ===
                            String(id)
                    );

            return (
                cashier?.full_name_ar ??
                cashier?.full_name ??
                cashier?.display_name ??
                cashier?.username ??
                "—"
            );

        }


        function resolveManagerName(
            id
        ){

            if (!id) return "—";

            // البحث أولاً في المديرين المؤهلين
            let manager =
                state.options.qualifiedManagers
                    .find(
                        x =>
                            String(
                                x.id ??
                                x.employee_id
                            ) ===
                            String(id)
                    );

            if (manager) {
                return manager.full_name_ar ?? manager.full_name ?? manager.name ?? manager.employee_name ?? "—";
            }

            // إذا لم يوجد، البحث في قائمة المديرين الكاملة
            manager =
                state.options.managers
                    .find(
                        x =>
                            String(
                                x.id ??
                                x.employee_id
                            ) ===
                            String(id)
                    );

            return (
                manager?.full_name_ar ??
                manager?.full_name ??
                manager?.name ??
                manager?.employee_name ??
                "—"
            );

        }


        /* =========================================================
           DETAILS
        ========================================================= */

        function openDetails(
            row
        ){

            if(!row){

                return;

            }


            const labels = {

                shift_code:
                    "رمز الوردية",

                station_id:
                    "المحطة",

                shift_date:
                    "تاريخ الوردية",

                shift_type:
                    "نوع الوردية",

                start_time:
                    "وقت البداية",

                end_time:
                    "وقت النهاية",

                duration_minutes:
                    "مدة الوردية",

                manager_id:
                    "مدير الوردية",

                cashier_id:
                    "أمين الصندوق",

                attendant_ids:
                    "العاملون",

                opening_cash:
                    "النقدية الافتتاحية",

                opening_bank:
                    "الرصيد البنكي الافتتاحي",

                opening_credit:
                    "الائتمان الافتتاحي",

                closing_cash:
                    "النقدية الختامية",

                closing_bank:
                    "الرصيد البنكي الختامي",

                closing_credit:
                    "الائتمان الختامي",

                total_sales:
                    "إجمالي المبيعات",

                total_fuel_sales:
                    "مبيعات الوقود",

                total_product_sales:
                    "مبيعات المنتجات",

                total_service_sales:
                    "مبيعات الخدمات",

                total_discounts:
                    "الخصومات",

                total_tax:
                    "الضريبة",

                total_vat:
                    "ضريبة القيمة المضافة",

                total_cash:
                    "المبيعات النقدية",

                total_credit_card:
                    "مبيعات البطاقات",

                total_bank_transfer:
                    "التحويلات البنكية",

                total_credit_sales:
                    "المبيعات الآجلة",

                total_cheque:
                    "الشيكات",

                total_other:
                    "وسائل الدفع الأخرى",

                total_fuel_liters:
                    "لترات الوقود",

                cash_variance:
                    "الانحراف النقدي",

                variance_reason:
                    "سبب الانحراف"

            };


            $("detailsBody")
                .innerHTML =
                `
                <div class="shift-grid">

                    ${Object.entries(row)
                        .map(
                            (
                                [
                                    key,
                                    value
                                ]
                            ) => {

                                let display =
                                    value;

                                if(
                                    key ===
                                    "station_id"
                                ){
                                    display =
                                        row.station_name_ar ||
                                        row.station_name ||
                                        resolveStationName(value);
                                }

                                if(key === "station_name" || key === "station_name_ar") return "";

                                if(key === "manager_id"){
                                    display =
                                        row.manager_name_ar ||
                                        row.manager_name ||
                                        resolveManagerName(value);
                                }

                                if(key === "manager_name" || key === "manager_name_ar") return "";

                                if(key === "cashier_id"){
                                    display =
                                        row.cashier_name_ar ||
                                        row.cashier_name ||
                                        resolveCashierName(value);
                                }

                                if(key === "cashier_name" || key === "cashier_name_ar") return "";

                                if(
                                    key ===
                                    "shift_type"
                                ){

                                    display =
                                        translateShiftType(
                                            value
                                        );

                                }

                                if(key === "attendant_ids"){
                                    display =
                                        row.attendant_names_ar ||
                                        row.attendant_names ||
                                        String(value ?? "") || "—";
                                }

                                if(key === "attendant_names" || key === "attendant_names_ar") return "";

                                return `
                                    <div class="info">

                                        <label>
                                            ${esc(
                                                labels[key] ||
                                                key
                                            )}
                                        </label>

                                        <strong>
                                            ${esc(
                                                typeof display ===
                                                "object"
                                                    ? JSON.stringify(display)
                                                    : display
                                            )}
                                        </strong>

                                    </div>
                                `;

                            }
                        )
                        .join("")}

                </div>
                `;


            $("detailsOverlay")
                .classList
                .add("show");

        }


        /* =========================================================
           DELETE
        ========================================================= */

        async function deleteRow(
            row
        ){

            if(!row){

                return;

            }


            const id =
                idOf(row);


            if(!id){

                toast(
                    "معرف الوردية غير صالح.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;

            }


            if(
                !confirm(
                    "هل تريد حذف سجل الوردية فعلياً؟"
                )
            ){

                return;

            }


            loading(
                true,
                "جارٍ حذف الوردية..."
            );


            try{

                const result =
                    await invoke(
                        CURRENT_CONTRACTS.remove,
                        [
                            Number(id)
                        ]
                    );


                requireSuccess(result);

                toast(
                    "تم حذف الوردية بنجاح.",
                    "success"
                );


                await reloadData();


            }catch(error){

                toast(
                    error.message ||
                    "فشل حذف الوردية.",
                    "error"
                );

            }finally{

                loading(false);

            }

        }


        /* =========================================================
           CLOSE SHIFT
        ========================================================= */

        async function closeShift(
            row
        ){

            if(!row){

                return;

            }


            const id =
                idOf(row);


            if(!id){

                toast(
                    "معرف الوردية غير صالح.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;

            }


            if(
                !confirm(
                    "هل تريد إنهاء هذه الوردية الآن؟"
                )
            ){

                return;

            }


            const payload = {

                ...row,

                end_time:
                    row.end_time ||
                    nowLocal()

            };


            if(
                payload.start_time &&
                payload.end_time
            ){

                const minutes =
                    Math.round(
                        (
                            new Date(
                                payload.end_time
                            ) -
                            new Date(
                                payload.start_time
                            )
                        ) /
                        60000
                    );

                if(
                    minutes >= 0
                ){

                    payload.duration_minutes =
                        minutes;

                }

            }


            loading(
                true,
                "جارٍ إنهاء الوردية..."
            );


            try{

                const result =
                    await invoke(
                        CURRENT_CONTRACTS.update,
                        [
                            Number(id),
                            JSON.stringify(
                                payload
                            )
                        ]
                    );


                requireSuccess(result);

                toast(
                    "تم إنهاء الوردية بنجاح.",
                    "success"
                );


                await reloadData();


            }catch(error){

                toast(
                    error.message ||
                    "فشل إنهاء الوردية.",
                    "error"
                );

            }finally{

                loading(false);

            }

        }


        /* =========================================================
           LOAD
        ========================================================= */

        async function reloadData(){

            if(
                !hasContract(
                    CURRENT_CONTRACTS.list
                )
            ){

                $("records")
                    .innerHTML =
                    `
                    <div class="error-state">

                        <i class="fas fa-triangle-exclamation"></i>

                        <span>
                            عقد تحميل الورديات غير متاح في Android.
                        </span>

                    </div>
                    `;

                return;

            }


            loading(
                true,
                "جارٍ تحميل الورديات..."
            );


            try{

                const result =
                    await invoke(
                        CURRENT_CONTRACTS.list,
                        [
                            JSON.stringify({

                                limit:
                                    200,

                                search:
                                    $("searchInput")
                                        .value ||
                                    ""

                            })
                        ]
                    );


                state.rows =
                    rowsFrom(
                        result
                    );


                state.loadedAt =
                    new Date();


                applyFilter();


            }catch(error){

                state.rows =
                    [];

                state.filtered =
                    [];

                render();


                toast(
                    error.message ||
                    "فشل تحميل الورديات.",
                    "error"
                );

            }finally{

                loading(false);

            }

        }


        /* =========================================================
           REPORT
        ========================================================= */

        function openReport(){

            $("reportFrom")
                .value =
                $("reportFrom")
                    .value ||
                today();


            $("reportTo")
                .value =
                $("reportTo")
                    .value ||
                today();


            $("reportResults")
                .innerHTML =
                `
                <div class="empty">

                    <i class="fas fa-chart-column"></i>

                    <strong>
                        التقرير جاهز
                    </strong>

                    <small>
                        حدد الفترة ثم اضغط توليد التقرير.
                    </small>

                </div>
                `;


            $("reportOverlay")
                .classList
                .add(
                    "show"
                );

        }


        async function generateReport(){

            const from =
                $("reportFrom")
                    .value;

            const to =
                $("reportTo")
                    .value;


            if(
                from &&
                to &&
                from > to
            ){

                toast(
                    "تاريخ البداية يجب ألا يكون بعد تاريخ النهاية.",
                    "error"
                );

                return;

            }


            loading(
                true,
                "جارٍ توليد التقرير..."
            );


            try{

                const payload = {

                    from_date:
                        from || "",

                    to_date:
                        to || "",

                    search:
                        $("searchInput")
                            .value ||
                        "",

                    include_archived:
                        true,

                    limit:
                        1000

                };


                const result =
                    await invoke(
                        CURRENT_CONTRACTS.report,
                        [
                            JSON.stringify(
