// ============================================================
let allMovements = [];
let allProducts = [];
let allFuelTypes = [];
let allTanks = [];
let allWarehouses = [];
let allSuppliers = [];
let allCustomers = [];
let chartInstance = null;
let currentPage = 1;
const pageSize = 20;
let activeMovementFilters = {};

// ============================================================
// 4. دوال التهيئة والتبويبات
// ============================================================
function switchTab(tabId) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-tab').forEach(t => { t.classList.remove('active'); t.setAttribute('aria-selected','false'); });
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
    const activeTab = document.querySelector(`.nav-tab[data-tab="${tabId}"]`);
    if (activeTab) { activeTab.classList.add('active'); activeTab.setAttribute('aria-selected','true'); }
    const content = document.getElementById(`${tabId}-tab`);
    if (content) content.classList.add('active');
    if (tabId === 'analytics') loadAnalytics();
    if (tabId === 'reports') loadReportDefaults();
}

function toggleTheme(theme) {
    const moon = document.getElementById('moonIcon');
    const sun = document.getElementById('sunIcon');
    const dark = theme === 'dark';
    if (dark) document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
    else document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');
    [sun, moon].forEach(el => el?.classList.remove('active-theme'));
    (dark ? moon : sun)?.classList.add('active-theme');
    localStorage.setItem('theme', dark ? 'dark' : 'light');
}

function restoreTheme() {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'light';
    toggleTheme(saved === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light');
}

function goToDashboard() {
    window.location.href = '../main.html';
}

// ============================================================
// 5. تحميل البيانات الأساسية
// ============================================================
async function loadInitialData() {
    showLoading(true);
    const results = await Promise.allSettled([
        loadProducts(),
        loadFuelTypes(),
        loadTanks(),
        loadWarehouses(),
        loadSuppliers(),
        loadCustomers(),
        loadActiveCashBoxes()
    ]);
    results.forEach((r, i) => {
        if (r.status === 'rejected') console.warn('Initial data item failed at index', i, r.reason);
    });
    try {
        await loadMovements();
        await loadStats();
        await loadAnalytics();
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error loading movements/stats:', e);
    } finally {
        showLoading(false);
    }
}

async function loadProducts() {
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getProducts', {});
        if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error || 'تعذر تحميل المنتجات من SQLite');
        allProducts = bridgeDataArray(result);
    } catch (e) {
        allProducts = [];
        console.error('loadProducts failed:', e);
    }
    renderProductPicker();
    return allProducts;
}

async function loadFuelTypes() {
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getFuelTypes', {});
        if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.error || 'تعذر تحميل أنواع الوقود من SQLite');
        allFuelTypes = bridgeDataArray(result);
    } catch (e) {
        allFuelTypes = [];
        console.error('loadFuelTypes failed:', e);
    }
    renderFuelPicker();
    return allFuelTypes;
}

async function loadTanks() {
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getTanks', {});
        if (result.success) {
            allTanks = bridgeDataArray(result);
            const filter = document.getElementById('filter-tank');
            if (filter) {
                filter.innerHTML = '<option value="">جميع الخزانات</option>';
                allTanks.forEach(t => {
                    const option = document.createElement('option');
                    option.value = t.tank_id || t.id;
                    option.textContent = `${t.tank_name || t.tank_code || ''} — ${t.fuel_name_ar || t.fuel_name || ''}`;
                    filter.appendChild(option);
                });
            }
        }
    } catch (e) { console.error('Error loading tanks:', e); }
}

async function loadWarehouses() {
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getWarehouses', {});
        if (result.success) {
            allWarehouses = bridgeDataArray(result);
            if (!allWarehouses.length) {
                try { await apiCall('ensureOperationalWarehouse', {}); } catch (_) {}
                const refreshed = await apiCall('getWarehouses', {});
                allWarehouses = bridgeDataArray(refreshed);
            }
            populateWarehouseDropdowns();
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error loading warehouses:', e);
    }
}

function populateWarehouseDropdowns() {
    const configs = [
        { id: 'filter-warehouse', defaultLabel: 'جميع المستودعات' },
        { id: 'source-warehouse', defaultLabel: 'اختر المستودع المصدر' },
        { id: 'target-warehouse', defaultLabel: 'اختر المستودع الهدف' }
    ];
    configs.forEach(cfg => {
        const sel = document.getElementById(cfg.id);
        if (!sel) return;
        const currentVal = sel.value;
        sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">${cfg.defaultLabel}</option>`;
        allWarehouses.forEach(w => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = w.warehouse_id || w.id;
            opt.textContent = w.warehouse_name || w.name || '';
            sel.appendChild(opt);
        });
        if (currentVal) sel.value = currentVal;
    });
}

async function loadSuppliers() {
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getSuppliers', {});
        if (result.success) {
            allSuppliers = bridgeDataArray(result);
            populatePartyDropdowns('filter-supplier', 'supplier-select', allSuppliers, 'جميع الموردين', 'اختر المورد');
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error loading suppliers:', e);
    }
}

async function loadCustomers() {
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getCustomers', {});
        if (result.success) {
            allCustomers = bridgeDataArray(result);
            populatePartyDropdowns('filter-customer', 'customer-select', allCustomers, 'جميع العملاء', 'اختر العميل');
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error loading customers:', e);
    }
}

async function loadActiveCashBoxes() {
    const select = document.getElementById('purchase-cashbox');
    if (!select) return;
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getActiveCashBoxes', {});
        if (!result || result.success !== true) throw new Error(result?.error || 'تعذر تحميل الصناديق');
        const rows = bridgeDataArray(result);
        const selected = select.value;
        select.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر صندوق الدفع</option>';
        rows.forEach(box => {
            const option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = String(box.id || box.cash_box_id || '');
            option.textContent = box.box_name_ar || box.box_name || box.box_code || ('صندوق #' + option.value);
            select.appendChild(option);
        });
        if (selected) select.value = selected;
    } catch (e) {
        select.innerHTML = '<option value="">تعذر تحميل الصناديق</option>';
        console.error('loadActiveCashBoxes failed:', e);
    }
}

function populatePartyDropdowns(filterId, selectId, list, filterDefaultLabel, selectDefaultLabel) {
    const filterSel = document.getElementById(filterId);
    if (filterSel) {
        const currentFilter = filterSel.value;
        filterSel.innerHTML = `<option value="">${filterDefaultLabel}</option>`;
        list.forEach(p => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = p.entity_id || p.id || p.party_id;
            opt.textContent = p.commercial_name_ar || p.commercial_name || p.legal_name || p.name || 'غير محدد';
            filterSel.appendChild(opt);
        });
        if (currentFilter) filterSel.value = currentFilter;
    }
    const sel = document.getElementById(selectId);
    if (!sel) return;
    const currentVal = sel.value;
    sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">${selectDefaultLabel}</option>`;
    list.forEach(p => {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = p.entity_id || p.id || p.party_id;
        opt.textContent = p.commercial_name_ar || p.commercial_name || p.legal_name || p.name || 'غير محدد';
        sel.appendChild(opt);
    });
    if (currentVal) sel.value = currentVal;
}

// ============================================================
// 6. تحميل الحركات والإحصائيات
// ============================================================
async function loadMovements() {
    showLoading(true);
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getMovements', { ...activeMovementFilters, page: currentPage, limit: pageSize });
        if (!result.success) throw result;
        const page = (result.data && typeof result.data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(result.data)) ? result.data : result;
        allMovements = movementRows(result);
        populateMovementsTable(allMovements);
        updatePagination(Number(page.total_count ?? page.total ?? result.total_count ?? result.total ?? allMovements.length));
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error loading movements:', e);
        showToast(e.error || 'خطأ في تحميل الحركات من SQLite', 'error');
    } finally { showLoading(false); }
}

function populateMovementsTable(movements) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('movements-table-body');
    if (!tbody) return;
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (!movements || movements.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="12" class="text-center">❌ لا توجد حركات لعرضها</td></tr>';
        return;
    }
    const pageOffset = (currentPage - 1) * pageSize;
    movements.forEach((m, idx) => {
        const row = document.createElement('tr');
        row.setAttribute('data-id', m.movement_id || m.id);
        row.onclick = function() { selectRow(this); };
        const isFuel = String(m.stock_type || '').toLowerCase() === 'fuel';
        const typeLabel = getMovementTypeLabel(m.movement_type, m.movement_subtype);
        const productOrFuel = `${isFuel ? '⛽ ' : '🛒 '}${escapeHtml(m.item_name || '---')}`;
        const location = m.location_name || '---';
        const status = m.status || 'completed';
        const statusBadge = status === 'completed' ? '<span class="badge badge-success">✅ مكتمل</span>' :
            status === 'pending' || status === 'in_progress' ? '<span class="badge badge-warning">⏳ قيد الانتظار</span>' :
            status === 'cancelled' ? '<span class="badge badge-danger">❌ ملغى</span>' :
            '<span class="badge badge-info">' + escapeHtml(status) + '</span>';
        // ترقيم متسلسل صحيح عبر الصفحات: pageOffset + idx + 1
        const serialNumber = pageOffset + idx + 1;
        // زر الحذف لا يظهر لحركات الوقود لأن archiveStockMovement يعمل على inventory_movements فقط
        const deleteButton = isFuel ? '' :
            `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteMovement(${Number(m.movement_id || m.id)})" title="أرشفة"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>`;
        row.innerHTML = `
          <td class="numeric">${serialNumber}</td>
          <td class="numeric">${escapeHtml(String(m.movement_date || '---').slice(0,19).replace('T',' '))}</td>
          <td>${productOrFuel}</td>
          <td class="numeric">${Number(m.quantity || m.quantity_change || 0).toLocaleString('en', {maximumFractionDigits:3})}</td>
          <td>${typeLabel}</td>
          <td>${escapeHtml(location)}</td>
          <td>${escapeHtml([m.source_location, m.target_location].filter(Boolean).join(' → ') || '---')}</td>
          <td>${escapeHtml(m.party_name || m.performed_by_name || '---')}</td>
          <td class="numeric">${Number(m.unit_cost || 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td class="numeric">${Number(m.total_cost || 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td>${statusBadge}</td>
          <td><div class="btn-action-group">
            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();viewMovement(${Number(m.movement_id || m.id)}, '${isFuel ? 'fuel' : 'product'}')" title="عرض"><i class="fas fa-eye"></i></button>
            ${deleteButton}
          </div></td>`;
        tbody.appendChild(row);
    });
}

function selectRow(row) {
    document.querySelectorAll('#movements-table-body tr').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('selected'));
    row.classList.add('selected');
}

function getMovementTypeLabel(type, subtype = '') {
    const labels = {
        in: '<span class="badge badge-success status-indicator">دخول مخزني</span>',
        out: `<span class="badge badge-danger status-indicator">${subtype === 'return_supplier' ? 'إرجاع لمورد' : (subtype === 'fuel_sale' ? 'صرف وقود' : 'خروج مخزني')}</span>`,
        return: '<span class="badge badge-pink status-indicator">إرجاع من عميل</span>',
        damage: '<span class="badge badge-cyan status-indicator">هالك / تلف</span>',
        transfer: '<span class="badge badge-info status-indicator">تحويل بين مستودعات</span>',
        adjustment: '<span class="badge badge-indigo status-indicator">تسوية مخزنية</span>',
        refill: '<span class="badge badge-success status-indicator">تعبئة خزان</span>',
        fuel_sale: '<span class="badge badge-danger status-indicator">صرف وقود</span>'
    };
    return labels[type] || `<span class="badge badge-secondary">${escapeHtml(type || 'غير محدد')}</span>`;
}

function updatePagination(total) {
    const container = document.getElementById('pagination-container');
    if (!container) return;
    const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / pageSize);
    if (totalPages <= 1) { container.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    let html = `<li class="page-item ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''}">
        <a class="page-link" href="#" onclick="event.preventDefault();changePage(${currentPage-1})">⬅️ السابق</a>
    </li>`;
    const maxShown = 5;
    let start = Math.max(1, currentPage - 2);
    let end = Math.min(totalPages, start + maxShown - 1);
    if (end - start + 1 < maxShown) start = Math.max(1, end - maxShown + 1);
    for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {
        html += `<li class="page-item ${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}">
            <a class="page-link" href="#" onclick="event.preventDefault();changePage(${i})">${i}</a>
        </li>`;
    }
    if (end < totalPages) html += `<li class="page-item disabled"><a class="page-link">...</a></li>`;
    html += `<li class="page-item ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''}">
        <a class="page-link" href="#" onclick="event.preventDefault();changePage(${currentPage+1})">➡️ التالي</a>
    </li>`;
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function changePage(page) {
    if (page < 1 || page === currentPage) return;
    currentPage = page;
    loadMovements();
}

async function loadStats() {
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getMovementStats', { ...activeMovementFilters });
        if (result.success) {
            const s = bridgeDataObject(result);
            const totalMovements = document.getElementById('total-movements');
            const totalValue = document.getElementById('total-value');
            const todayMovements = document.getElementById('today-movements');
            const pendingCount = document.getElementById('pending-count');
            if (totalMovements) totalMovements.textContent = toNumber(s.total_movements ?? s.movement_count ?? s.total_count).toLocaleString('en');
            if (totalValue) totalValue.textContent = toNumber(s.total_value ?? s.movement_value ?? s.total_cost).toLocaleString('en', {minimumFractionDigits:2, maximumFractionDigits:2});
            if (todayMovements) todayMovements.textContent = toNumber(s.today_movements ?? s.today_count).toLocaleString('en');
            if (pendingCount) pendingCount.textContent = toNumber(s.pending_count ?? s.pending_movements).toLocaleString('en');
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error loading stats:', e);
    }
}

// ============================================================
// 7. دوال الفلترة
// ============================================================
function onMovementStockTypeChanged() {
    const type = document.getElementById('filter-stock-type').value;
    const productGroup = document.getElementById('product-filter-group');
    const fuelGroup = document.getElementById('fuel-filter-group');
    const tankGroup = document.getElementById('filter-tank-group');
    const typeGroup = document.getElementById('filter-type-group');
    const warehouseGroup = document.getElementById('filter-warehouse-group');

    // عند "الكل": إخفاء حقول (بحث بالمنتج) و (بحث بالوقود) و (نوع الحركة) و (الخزان) و (المستودع)
    if (productGroup) productGroup.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type !== 'products');
    if (fuelGroup) fuelGroup.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type !== 'fuel');
    if (tankGroup) tankGroup.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type !== 'fuel');
    if (typeGroup) typeGroup.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type === 'all');
    if (warehouseGroup) warehouseGroup.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type === 'fuel');

    // تصفير الحقول المخفية
    if (type !== 'products') {
        const el = document.getElementById('filter-product-id'); if (el) el.value = '';
        const el2 = document.getElementById('search-product'); if (el2) el2.value = '';
    }
    if (type !== 'fuel') {
        const el = document.getElementById('filter-fuel-id'); if (el) el.value = '';
        const el2 = document.getElementById('search-fuel'); if (el2) el2.value = '';
        const el3 = document.getElementById('filter-tank'); if (el3) el3.value = '';
    }
    // إعادة بناء أنواع الحركة حسب نوع المخزون
    populateFilterTypeOptions(type);
}

function populateFilterTypeOptions(stockType) {
    const sel = document.getElementById('filter-type');
    if (!sel) return;
    const current = sel.value;
    let options = '';
    if (stockType === 'fuel') {
        options = `<option value="">جميع الأنواع</option>
            <option value="in">⛽ استلام / تعبئة</option>
            <option value="out">🚚 صرف / استهلاك</option>
            <option value="refill">🛢️ تعبئة خزان</option>
            <option value="adjustment">⚖️ تسوية رصيد</option>`;
    } else {
        options = `<option value="">جميع الأنواع</option>
            <option value="in">📥 دخول مخزني</option>
            <option value="out">📤 خروج مخزني</option>
            <option value="return_customer">🔄 إرجاع من عميل</option>
            <option value="return_supplier">🔄 إرجاع لمورد</option>
            <option value="waste">🗑️ هالك</option>
            <option value="transfer">🔄 تحويل بين مستودعات</option>
            <option value="adjustment">⚖️ تسوية مخزنية</option>
            <option value="stocktake">📋 جرد</option>`;
    }
    sel.innerHTML = options;
    if ([...sel.options].some(o => o.value === current)) sel.value = current;
}

function applyFilters() {
    const stockType = document.getElementById('filter-stock-type').value;
    const selectedType = document.getElementById('filter-type').value;
    const type = ({ return_customer: 'return', return_supplier: 'out', waste: 'damage', stocktake: 'adjustment' })[selectedType] || selectedType;
    const filters = {
        stock_type: stockType,
        movement_type: type,
        start_date: document.getElementById('filter-from').value,
        end_date: document.getElementById('filter-to').value,
        warehouse_id: stockType === 'products' ? Number(document.getElementById('filter-warehouse').value || 0) : 0,
        tank_id: stockType === 'fuel' ? Number(document.getElementById('filter-tank').value || 0) : 0,
        status: document.getElementById('filter-status').value,
        supplier_id: Number(document.getElementById('filter-supplier').value || 0),
        customer_id: Number(document.getElementById('filter-customer').value || 0)
    };
    const selectedItemId = stockType === 'products'
        ? Number(document.getElementById('filter-product-id').value || 0)
        : Number(document.getElementById('filter-fuel-id').value || 0);
    filters.item_id = selectedItemId;
    filters.product_id = stockType === 'products' ? selectedItemId : 0;
    filters.fuel_type_id = stockType === 'fuel' ? selectedItemId : 0;
    filters.query = stockType === 'products'
        ? document.getElementById('search-product').value.trim()
        : document.getElementById('search-fuel').value.trim();
    activeMovementFilters = filters;
    currentPage = 1;
    Promise.all([loadMovements(), loadStats()]);
}

function resetFilters() {
    document.getElementById('filter-stock-type').value = 'all';
    const ids = ['filter-product-id','filter-fuel-id','search-product','search-fuel','filter-tank','filter-type','filter-from','filter-to','filter-warehouse','filter-supplier','filter-customer','filter-status'];
    ids.forEach(id => { const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = ''; });
    onMovementStockTypeChanged();
    activeMovementFilters = {};
    currentPage = 1;
    Promise.all([loadMovements(), loadStats()]);
    showToast('تم إعادة ضبط الفلترة', 'success');
}

// ============================================================
// 8. دوال المودال والحركات
// ============================================================
function toggleMovementStockType() {
    const type = document.getElementById('movement-stock-type').value;
    const productGroup = document.getElementById('product-selector-group');
    const fuelGroup = document.getElementById('fuel-movement-fields');
    const tankGroup = document.getElementById('tank-group');
    const productInfo = document.getElementById('product-info');
    const typeSelect = document.getElementById('movement-type');

    if (productGroup) productGroup.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type !== 'products');
    if (fuelGroup) fuelGroup.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type !== 'fuel');
    if (tankGroup) tankGroup.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type !== 'fuel');
    if (productInfo) productInfo.style.display = 'none';

    if (type === 'products') {
        const fuelSel = document.getElementById('fuel-select');
        const fuelSelName = document.getElementById('fuel-select-display');
        const tankSel = document.getElementById('tank-select');
        if (fuelSel) fuelSel.value = '';
        if (fuelSelName) fuelSelName.value = '';
        if (tankSel) {
            tankSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر الخزان</option>';
            tankSel.value = '';
        }
        typeSelect.innerHTML = `<option value="">اختر نوع الحركة</option>
          <option value="in">📥 دخول مخزني (شراء)</option>
          <option value="out">📤 خروج مخزني (بيع)</option>
          <option value="return_customer">🔄 إرجاع من عميل</option>
          <option value="return_supplier">🔄 إرجاع لمورد</option>
          <option value="waste">🗑️ هالك</option>
          <option value="transfer">🔄 تحويل بين مستودعات</option>
          <option value="adjustment">⚖️ تسوية مخزنية</option>
          <option value="stocktake">📋 جرد</option>`;
    } else {
        const productSel = document.getElementById('product-select');
        const productDisplay = document.getElementById('product-select-display');
        if (productSel) productSel.value = '';
        if (productDisplay) productDisplay.value = '';
        typeSelect.innerHTML = `<option value="">اختر نوع الحركة</option>
          <option value="in">⛽ استلام / تعبئة الخزان</option>
          <option value="out">🚚 صرف / استهلاك الوقود</option>
          <option value="adjustment">⚖️ تسوية رصيد الخزان</option>`;
    }
    toggleMovementFields();
}

function toggleMovementFields() {
    const type = document.getElementById('movement-type').value;
    const stockType = document.getElementById('movement-stock-type').value;
    ['source-warehouse-group', 'target-warehouse-group', 'supplier-group', 'customer-group',
     'adjustment-direction-group', 'purchase-payment-group', 'purchase-cashbox-group']
        .forEach(id => { const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.classList.add('hidden-field'); });

    if (stockType === 'products' && type === 'in') {
        document.getElementById('purchase-payment-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        if (document.getElementById('purchase-payment-method')?.value === 'cash') {
            document.getElementById('purchase-cashbox-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        }
    }

    if (stockType === 'fuel') {
        if (type === 'adjustment') {
            const el = document.getElementById('adjustment-direction-group');
            if (el) el.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        }
        return;
    }
    if (type === 'in') {
        document.getElementById('target-warehouse-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        document.getElementById('supplier-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
    } else if (type === 'return_customer') {
        document.getElementById('target-warehouse-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        document.getElementById('customer-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
    } else if (type === 'out') {
        document.getElementById('source-warehouse-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        document.getElementById('customer-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
    } else if (type === 'return_supplier') {
        document.getElementById('source-warehouse-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        document.getElementById('supplier-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
    } else if (type === 'waste') {
        document.getElementById('source-warehouse-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
    } else if (type === 'transfer') {
        document.getElementById('source-warehouse-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        document.getElementById('target-warehouse-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
    } else if (type === 'adjustment' || type === 'stocktake') {
        document.getElementById('source-warehouse-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
        document.getElementById('adjustment-direction-group')?.classList.remove('hidden-field');
    }
    const defaultWarehouseId = allWarehouses.length === 1 ? String(allWarehouses[0].warehouse_id || allWarehouses[0].id || '') : '';
    if (defaultWarehouseId) {
        if (['in', 'return_customer', 'transfer'].includes(type)) {
            const el = document.getElementById('target-warehouse');
            if (el && !el.value) el.value = defaultWarehouseId;
        }
        if (['out', 'return_supplier', 'waste', 'adjustment', 'stocktake', 'transfer'].includes(type)) {
            const el = document.getElementById('source-warehouse');
            if (el && !el.value) el.value = defaultWarehouseId;
        }
    }
}

async function openProductPicker(target) {
    window.productPickerTarget = target;
    const search = document.getElementById('productPickerSearch');
    if (search) search.value = '';
    try {
        await loadProducts();
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Product picker SQLite load failed:', e);
        showToast(e.message || 'تعذر تحميل المنتجات من SQLite', 'error');
    }
    renderProductPicker();
    if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
        window.bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(document.getElementById('productPickerModal')).show();
    }
}

function renderProductPicker() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('productPickerSearch')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const body = document.getElementById('productPickerBody');
    if (!body) return;
    const rows = allProducts.filter(p => {
        const text = `${p.product_name || p.name || ''} ${p.product_name_ar || ''} ${p.product_code || p.code || ''} ${p.barcode || ''}`.toLowerCase();
        return !q || text.includes(q);
    });
    body.innerHTML = rows.slice(0, 200).map(p => {
        const id = Number(p.product_id || p.id);
        const name = p.product_name_ar || p.product_name || p.name || '---';
        return `<tr class="picker-row" data-id="${id}" onclick="markPickerRow(this)" ondblclick="selectProduct(${id})">
            <td>${escapeHtml(name)}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(p.product_code || p.code || '---')}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(p.barcode || '---')}</td>
            <td><button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" type="button" onclick="event.stopPropagation();selectProduct(${id})">اختيار</button></td>
        </tr>`;
    }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center">لا توجد منتجات مطابقة</td></tr>';
}

function markPickerRow(row) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.picker-row').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('dbl-selected'));
    row.classList.add('dbl-selected');
}

function selectProduct(id) {
    const p = allProducts.find(x => Number(x.product_id || x.id) === Number(id));
    if (!p) return;
    const name = p.product_name_ar || p.product_name || p.name || '---';
    const target = window.productPickerTarget;
    if (target === 'create') {
        const hidden = document.getElementById('product-select');
        const display = document.getElementById('product-select-display');
        if (hidden) hidden.value = String(id);
        if (display) display.value = name;
    }
    if (target === 'filter') {
        const filterId = document.getElementById('filter-product-id');
        const searchBox = document.getElementById('search-product');
        if (filterId) filterId.value = String(id);
        if (searchBox) searchBox.value = name;
    }
    if (target === 'analytics') {
        const analyticsId = document.getElementById('analytics-product');
        const analyticsName = document.getElementById('analytics-product-name');
        if (analyticsId) analyticsId.value = String(id);
        if (analyticsName) analyticsName.value = name;
    }
    if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
        window.bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('productPickerModal'))?.hide();
    }
    if (target === 'create') loadProductInfo();
}

async function openFuelPicker(target) {
    window.fuelPickerTarget = target;
    const search = document.getElementById('fuelPickerSearch');
    if (search) search.value = '';
    try {
        await loadFuelTypes();
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Fuel picker SQLite load failed:', e);
        showToast(e.message || 'تعذر تحميل أنواع الوقود من SQLite', 'error');
    }
    renderFuelPicker();
    if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
        window.bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(document.getElementById('fuelPickerModal')).show();
    }
}

function renderFuelPicker() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('fuelPickerSearch')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const body = document.getElementById('fuelPickerBody');
    if (!body) return;
    const rows = allFuelTypes.filter(f => {
        const text = `${f.fuel_name_ar || ''} ${f.fuel_name || ''} ${f.fuel_code || ''}`.toLowerCase();
        return !q || text.includes(q);
    });
    body.innerHTML = rows.slice(0, 100).map(f => {
        const id = Number(f.fuel_type_id || f.id);
        const name = f.fuel_name_ar || f.fuel_name || '---';
        const price = Number(f.default_sale_price || f.default_purchase_price || 0).toFixed(2);
        return `<tr class="picker-row" data-id="${id}" onclick="markPickerRow(this)" ondblclick="selectFuel(${id})">
            <td>${escapeHtml(name)}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(f.fuel_code || '---')}</td>
            <td class="numeric">${price}</td>
            <td><button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" type="button" onclick="event.stopPropagation();selectFuel(${id})">اختيار</button></td>
        </tr>`;
    }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center">لا توجد أنواع وقود مطابقة</td></tr>';
}

function selectFuel(id) {
    const f = allFuelTypes.find(x => Number(x.fuel_type_id || x.id) === Number(id));
    if (!f) return;
    const name = f.fuel_name_ar || f.fuel_name || '---';
    const target = window.fuelPickerTarget;
    if (target === 'create') {
        const hidden = document.getElementById('fuel-select');
        const display = document.getElementById('fuel-select-display');
        if (hidden) hidden.value = String(id);
        if (display) display.value = name;
        // تصفية الخزانات حسب نوع الوقود المختار فقط
        const tank = document.getElementById('tank-select');
        if (tank) {
            tank.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر الخزان</option>';
            allTanks
                .filter(t => Number(t.fuel_type_id || t.fuelTypeId || 0) === Number(id))
                .forEach(t => {
                    const opt = document.createElement('option');
                    opt.value = t.tank_id || t.id;
                    opt.textContent = `${t.tank_name || t.tank_code || ''} — ${t.fuel_name_ar || t.fuel_name || ''}`;
                    tank.appendChild(opt);
                });
            if (tank.options.length === 2) tank.selectedIndex = 1;
        }
        const price = Number(f.default_sale_price || f.default_purchase_price || 0);
        if (price > 0) {
            const priceInput = document.getElementById('unit-price');
            if (priceInput) priceInput.value = price.toFixed(2);
        }
    } else if (target === 'filter') {
        const filterId = document.getElementById('filter-fuel-id');
        const searchBox = document.getElementById('search-fuel');
        if (filterId) filterId.value = String(id);
        if (searchBox) searchBox.value = name;
    } else if (target === 'analytics') {
        const analyticsId = document.getElementById('analytics-fuel');
        const analyticsName = document.getElementById('analytics-fuel-name');
        if (analyticsId) analyticsId.value = String(id);
        if (analyticsName) analyticsName.value = name;
    }
    if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
        window.bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('fuelPickerModal'))?.hide();
    }
}

function toggleAnalyticsSelector() {
    const type = document.getElementById('analytics-stock-type').value;
    document.getElementById('analytics-product-group')?.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type !== 'products');
    document.getElementById('analytics-fuel-group')?.classList.toggle('hidden-field', type !== 'fuel');
    if (type === 'products') {
        const el = document.getElementById('analytics-fuel'); if (el) el.value = '';
        const el2 = document.getElementById('analytics-fuel-name'); if (el2) el2.value = '';
    } else {
        const el = document.getElementById('analytics-product'); if (el) el.value = '';
        const el2 = document.getElementById('analytics-product-name'); if (el2) el2.value = '';
    }
}

function setValue(id, value) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.value = value;
}

async function showCreateModal() {
    const modalElement = document.getElementById('createModal');
    if (!modalElement) {
        showToast('تعذر فتح نافذة إضافة الحركة: عنصر النافذة غير موجود', 'error');
        return;
    }

    // تهيئة النموذج قبل العرض
    setValue('movement-stock-type', 'products');
    setValue('movement-type', '');
    setValue('movement-date', new Date().toISOString().slice(0,16));
    ['product-select','product-select-display','fuel-select','fuel-select-display','tank-select','movement-quantity','unit-price',
     'source-warehouse','target-warehouse','supplier-select','customer-select','movement-reference','movement-notes']
        .forEach(id => setValue(id, ''));
    const productInfo = document.getElementById('product-info');
    if (productInfo) productInfo.style.display = 'none';

    if (typeof toggleMovementStockType === 'function') toggleMovementStockType();

    try {
        if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
            window.bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(modalElement).show();
        } else {
            modalElement.classList.add('show');
            modalElement.style.display = 'block';
            modalElement.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
            modalElement.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
            document.body.classList.add('modal-open');
            let backdrop = document.getElementById('create-modal-backdrop');
            if (!backdrop) {
                backdrop = document.createElement('div');
                backdrop.id = 'create-modal-backdrop';
                backdrop.className = 'modal-backdrop fade show';
                backdrop.addEventListener('click', () => closeCreateModal());
                document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
            }
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error showing create movement modal:', error);
        showToast('تعذر فتح نافذة إضافة الحركة', 'error');
        return;
    }

    Promise.allSettled([loadProducts(), loadWarehouses(), loadFuelTypes(), loadTanks()]);
}

function closeCreateModal() {
    const modalElement = document.getElementById('createModal');
    if (!modalElement) return;
    if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
        window.bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(modalElement).hide();
        return;
    }
    modalElement.classList.remove('show');
    modalElement.style.display = 'none';
    modalElement.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    modalElement.removeAttribute('aria-modal');
    document.body.classList.remove('modal-open');
    document.getElementById('create-modal-backdrop')?.remove();
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    const button = document.getElementById('open-create-movement-button');
    if (!button) return;
    button.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        void showCreateModal();
    });
});

async function loadProductInfo() {
    const productId = Number(document.getElementById('product-select').value);
    if (!productId) {
        document.getElementById('product-info').style.display = 'none';
        return;
    }
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('getInventoryDetails', { product_id: productId });
        const d = result.data || result;
        document.getElementById('current-quantity').textContent = Number(d.quantity || 0).toLocaleString('en');
        document.getElementById('current-cost').textContent = Number(d.purchase_price || 0).toLocaleString('en', {minimumFractionDigits:2, maximumFractionDigits:2});
        document.getElementById('min-stock').textContent = Number(d.minimum_stock || 0).toLocaleString('en');
        const movementsArr = Array.isArray(d.movements) ? d.movements : [];
        document.getElementById('last-movement').textContent = movementsArr.length ? (movementsArr[0].movement_date || '---') : '---';
        const unitPriceEl = document.getElementById('unit-price');
        if (unitPriceEl && !unitPriceEl.value && Number(d.purchase_price || 0) > 0) {
            unitPriceEl.value = Number(d.purchase_price).toFixed(2);
        }
        document.getElementById('product-info').style.display = 'block';
    } catch (e) {
        document.getElementById('product-info').style.display = 'none';
        console.error('loadProductInfo failed:', e);
        showToast(e.error || 'تعذر تحميل رصيد المنتج', 'error');
    }
}

let activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey = '';
let productPurchaseKeySequence = 0;
const PRODUCT_PURCHASE_IDEMPOTENCY_STORAGE_KEY = 'product_purchase_idempotency_v1';
function productPurchaseIdempotencyKey(data = {}) {
    const fingerprint = JSON.stringify([
        String(data.supplier_id || ''), String(data.product_id || ''),
        String(data.warehouse_id || ''), Number(data.quantity || 0),
        Number(data.unit_cost || 0), String(data.payment_method || ''),
        String(data.cash_box_id || ''), String(data.movement_date || ''),
        String(data.reference_code || '')
    ]);
    try {
        const existing = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PRODUCT_PURCHASE_IDEMPOTENCY_STORAGE_KEY) || 'null');
        if (existing && existing.key && existing.fingerprint === fingerprint) {
            activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey = existing.key;
            return existing.key;
        }
        const token = (window.crypto && typeof window.crypto.randomUUID === 'function')
            ? window.crypto.randomUUID()
            : Date.now().toString(36) + '-' + (++productPurchaseKeySequence).toString(36);
        activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey = 'product-purchase-' + token;
        localStorage.setItem(PRODUCT_PURCHASE_IDEMPOTENCY_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
            key: activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey, fingerprint
        }));
    } catch (_) {
        if (!activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey) {
            const token = (window.crypto && typeof window.crypto.randomUUID === 'function')
                ? window.crypto.randomUUID()
                : Date.now().toString(36) + '-' + (++productPurchaseKeySequence).toString(36);
            activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey = 'product-purchase-' + token;
        }
    }
    return activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey;
}
function clearProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey(key) {
    if (activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey === key) activeProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey = '';
    try {
        const existing = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PRODUCT_PURCHASE_IDEMPOTENCY_STORAGE_KEY) || 'null');
        if (existing && existing.key === key) localStorage.removeItem(PRODUCT_PURCHASE_IDEMPOTENCY_STORAGE_KEY);
    } catch (_) {}
}

async function saveMovement() {
    const stockType = document.getElementById('movement-stock-type').value;
    const type = document.getElementById('movement-type').value;
    const quantity = Number(document.getElementById('movement-quantity').value);
    const date = document.getElementById('movement-date').value;
    const unitPrice = Number(document.getElementById('unit-price').value || 0);
    const reference = document.getElementById('movement-reference').value.trim();
    const notes = document.getElementById('movement-notes').value.trim();

    if (!type || !Number.isFinite(quantity) || quantity <= 0 || !date) {
        showToast('يرجى ملء الحقول المطلوبة بقيم صحيحة', 'error');
        return;
    }

    let data;
    if (stockType === 'fuel') {
        const fuelTypeId = Number(document.getElementById('fuel-select').value || 0);
        const tankId = Number(document.getElementById('tank-select').value || 0);
        if (!fuelTypeId) { showToast('اختر نوع الوقود من بيانات SQLite', 'error'); return; }
        if (!tankId) { showToast('اختر الخزان الذي ستُوثَّق إليه الحركة', 'error'); return; }
        data = {
            stock_type: 'fuel',
            movement_type: type,
            fuel_type_id: fuelTypeId,
            tank_id: tankId,
            quantity,
            unit_cost: unitPrice,
            movement_date: date,
            reference_code: reference,
            notes,
            adjustment_direction: document.getElementById('adjustment-direction').value
        };
    } else {
        const productId = Number(document.getElementById('product-select').value);
        const sourceWarehouse = Number(document.getElementById('source-warehouse').value || 0);
        const targetWarehouse = Number(document.getElementById('target-warehouse').value || 0);
        const supplierId = Number(document.getElementById('supplier-select').value || 0);
        const customerId = Number(document.getElementById('customer-select').value || 0);
        const purchasePaymentMethod = String(document.getElementById('purchase-payment-method')?.value || 'cash');
        const purchaseCashBoxId = Number(document.getElementById('purchase-cashbox')?.value || 0);
        if (!productId) { showToast('اختر المنتج من SQLite', 'error'); return; }
        if (type === 'in' && !supplierId) { showToast('اختر المورد لتوثيق فاتورة شراء المنتجات', 'error'); return; }
        if (type === 'in' && (!Number.isFinite(unitPrice) || unitPrice <= 0)) { showToast('أدخل تكلفة شراء صحيحة للمنتج', 'error'); return; }
        if (type === 'in' && purchasePaymentMethod === 'cash' && !purchaseCashBoxId) { showToast('اختر صندوق دفع المورد', 'error'); return; }
        if (type === 'transfer' && (!sourceWarehouse || !targetWarehouse || sourceWarehouse === targetWarehouse)) {
            showToast('اختر مستودعين مختلفين للتحويل', 'error'); return;
        }
        if (['in', 'return_customer'].includes(type) && !targetWarehouse) {
            showToast('اختر المستودع الهدف', 'error'); return;
        }
        if (['out', 'return_supplier', 'waste', 'adjustment', 'stocktake'].includes(type) && !sourceWarehouse) {
            showToast('اختر المستودع المصدر', 'error'); return;
        }
        const backendType = type === 'return_customer' ? 'return'
            : type === 'return_supplier' ? 'out'
            : type === 'waste' ? 'damage'
            : type === 'stocktake' ? 'adjustment'
            : type;
        const adjustmentDirectionEl = document.getElementById('adjustment-direction');
        const signedQuantity = ['adjustment', 'stocktake'].includes(type) && adjustmentDirectionEl && adjustmentDirectionEl.value === 'decrease'
            ? -quantity
            : quantity;
        data = {
            stock_type: 'products',
            movement_type: backendType,
            movement_subtype: type,
            product_id: productId,
            quantity,
            signed_quantity: signedQuantity,
            movement_date: date,
            unit_cost: unitPrice,
            warehouse_id: ['in','return_customer'].includes(type) ? targetWarehouse : sourceWarehouse,
            source_warehouse_id: sourceWarehouse || null,
            target_warehouse_id: targetWarehouse || null,
            supplier_id: supplierId || null,
            customer_id: customerId || null,
            financial_purchase: type === 'in',
            payment_method: type === 'in' ? purchasePaymentMethod : '',
            cash_box_id: type === 'in' && purchasePaymentMethod === 'cash' ? purchaseCashBoxId : 0,
            idempotency_key: '',
            reference_code: reference,
            reference_type: type,
            notes
        };
        if (type === 'in') data.idempotency_key = productPurchaseIdempotencyKey(data);
    }
    showLoading(true);
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('saveMovement', data);
        showToast(result.message || 'تم حفظ الحركة وتحديث الرصيد بنجاح', 'success');
        if (stockType === 'products' && type === 'in') clearProductPurchaseIdempotencyKey(data.idempotency_key);
        if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
            window.bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('createModal'))?.hide();
        } else {
            closeCreateModal();
        }
        await Promise.all([loadMovements(), loadStats(), loadTanks()]);
    } catch (e) {
        showToast(e.error || e.message || 'فشل الحفظ ولم يتم اعتماد الحركة', 'error');
    } finally {
        showLoading(false);
    }
}

// ============================================================
// 9. عرض تفاصيل الحركة من المصفوفة المحلية (بدون استعلام جديد)
// ============================================================
function viewMovement(id, hintStockType, skipRemoteRead = false) {
    const movementId = Number(id);
    if (!movementId) { showToast('معرف الحركة غير صالح', 'error'); return; }
    const details = document.getElementById('movement-details');
    if (!details) return;
    if (!skipRemoteRead) {
        details.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center">⏳ جاري تحميل تفاصيل الحركة من SQLite...</div>';
        if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
            window.bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(document.getElementById('viewModal')).show();
        }
    }
    // المصدر الأول: الصف المحلي، ثم تُستكمل البيانات من SQLite بالعقد المخصص للحركة.
    let m = allMovements.find(x => Number(x.movement_id || x.id) === movementId);
    if (!m) {
        if (skipRemoteRead) {
            details.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-warning">تعذر العثور على تفاصيل الحركة في نطاق المحطة الحالية.</div>';
            return;
        }
        invokeInventoryMovementDetailsAsync(movementId).then(result => {
            const rows = movementRows(result);
            if (!rows.length) throw new Error(result.error || result.message || 'لم يتم العثور على الحركة');
            allMovements = [rows[0], ...allMovements.filter(row => Number(row.movement_id || row.id) !== movementId)];
            viewMovement(movementId, hintStockType, true);
        }).catch(error => {
            details.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-danger">' + escapeHtml(error.message || 'فشل تحميل تفاصيل الحركة') + '</div>';
        });
        return;
    }
    const isFuel = String(m.stock_type || '').toLowerCase() === 'fuel';
    details.innerHTML = `<div class="info-grid">
        <div class="info-item"><span>نوع المخزون:</span><span>${isFuel ? '⛽ الوقود' : '🛒 المنتجات'}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>رقم الحركة:</span><span class="numeric">${escapeHtml(String(m.movement_id || m.id || '---'))}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>الصنف:</span><span>${escapeHtml(m.item_name || '---')}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>الكود:</span><span>${escapeHtml(m.item_code || '---')}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>نوع الحركة:</span><span>${getMovementTypeLabel(m.movement_type, m.movement_subtype)}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>التاريخ:</span><span class="numeric">${escapeHtml(String(m.movement_date || '---').slice(0,19).replace('T',' '))}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>الكمية:</span><span class="numeric">${Number(m.quantity || m.quantity_change || 0).toLocaleString('en', {maximumFractionDigits:3})}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>سعر الوحدة:</span><span class="numeric">${Number(m.unit_cost || 0).toFixed(2)}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>القيمة:</span><span class="numeric">${Number(m.total_cost || 0).toFixed(2)}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>الموقع/الخزان:</span><span>${escapeHtml(m.location_name || '---')}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>المسار:</span><span>${escapeHtml([m.source_location, m.target_location].filter(Boolean).join(' → ') || '---')}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>المرجع:</span><span>${escapeHtml(m.reference_code || '---')}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>الطرف:</span><span>${escapeHtml(m.party_name || '---')}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>المنفذ:</span><span>${escapeHtml(m.performed_by_name || '---')}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>الحالة:</span><span>${escapeHtml(m.status || '---')}</span></div>
        <div class="info-item"><span>الجدول المصدر:</span><span>${escapeHtml(m.source_table || '---')}</span></div>
    </div>`;
    if (window.bootstrap && window.bootstrap.Modal) {
        window.bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(document.getElementById('viewModal')).show();
    }
    if (!skipRemoteRead) {
        const requestVersion = ++inventoryMovementDetailsRequestVersion;
        invokeInventoryMovementDetailsAsync(movementId).then(result => {
            if (requestVersion !== inventoryMovementDetailsRequestVersion) return;
            const rows = movementRows(result);
            if (!rows.length) return;
            allMovements = [rows[0], ...allMovements.filter(row => Number(row.movement_id || row.id) !== movementId)];
            viewMovement(movementId, hintStockType, true);
        }).catch(error => {
            if (requestVersion !== inventoryMovementDetailsRequestVersion) return;
            console.warn('Inventory movement details refresh failed:', error.message || error);
        });
    }
}

// ============================================================
// 10. الأرشفة — فحص صريح لزر التأكيد (لا يُنفّذ عند الإلغاء)
// ============================================================
async function deleteMovement(id) {
    const movementId = Number(id);
    if (!movementId || movementId <= 0) { showToast('معرف الحركة غير صالح', 'error'); return; }

    // حركات الوقود لا تُؤرشف من هذه الشاشة (الجسر archiveStockMovement يعمل على inventory_movements)
    const movement = allMovements.find(x => Number(x.movement_id || x.id) === movementId);
    if (movement && String(movement.stock_type || '').toLowerCase() === 'fuel') {
        showToast('لا يمكن أرشفة حركات الوقود من هذه الشاشة', 'warning');
        return;
    }

    let confirmation;
    try {
        if (window.Swal && typeof Swal.fire === 'function') {
            confirmation = await Swal.fire({
                title: 'أرشفة الحركة؟',
                text: 'سيتم إخفاؤها من القوائم دون حذفها من سجل التدقيق.',
                icon: 'warning',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: 'نعم، أرشفة',
                cancelButtonText: 'إلغاء',
                reverseButtons: true,
                focusCancel: true,
                allowOutsideClick: false,
                allowEscapeKey: true
            });
        } else {
            confirmation = { isConfirmed: confirm('هل تريد أرشفة السجل المحدد؟') };
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Swal error:', e);
        confirmation = { isConfirmed: confirm('هل تريد أرشفة السجل المحدد؟') };
    }

    // فحص صريح ومزدوج: لا نكمل إلا إذا أكّد المستخدم بنعم صراحةً
    if (!confirmation || confirmation.isConfirmed !== true) {
        return;
    }

    showLoading(true);
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('deleteMovement', { movement_id: movementId });
        showToast(result.message || 'تمت الأرشفة بنجاح', 'success');
        await Promise.all([loadMovements(), loadStats()]);
    } catch (e) {
        showToast(e.error || e.message || 'فشلت الأرشفة ولم تتغير البيانات', 'error');
    } finally {
        showLoading(false);
    }
}

function printMovement() {
    const content = document.getElementById('movement-details').innerHTML;
    const win = window.open('', '_blank');
    if (!win) return;
    win.document.write(`
        <html dir="rtl"><head><title>تفاصيل الحركة</title>
        <meta charset="utf-8">
        <style>body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;direction:rtl;}
        .info-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;}
        .info-item{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #ddd;}</style>
        </head><body>
        <h2 style="text-align:center;">📊 تفاصيل الحركة المخزنية</h2>
        <div class="info-grid">${content}</div>
        <p style="text-align:center;margin-top:20px;color:#888;">تم الطباعة من نظام Station Enterprise V5</p>
        <script>window.onload=function(){window.print();}<\/script>
        </body></html>
    `);
    win.document.close();
}

// ============================================================
// 11. التقارير
// ============================================================
function loadReportDefaults() {
    const today = new Date();
    const weekAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 7*24*60*60*1000);
    const fmt = d => d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');
    const fromEl = document.getElementById('report-from');
    const toEl = document.getElementById('report-to');
    if (fromEl && !fromEl.value) fromEl.value = fmt(weekAgo);
    if (toEl && !toEl.value) toEl.value = fmt(today);
}

async function generateReport() {
    const type = document.getElementById('report-type').value;
    const from = document.getElementById('report-from').value;
    const to = document.getElementById('report-to').value;
    const stockType = document.getElementById('report-stock-type').value;
    showLoading(true);
    try {
        const params = { report_type: type, start_date: from, end_date: to, stock_type: stockType, limit: 5000, page: 1 };
        const result = await apiCall('generateReport', params);
        if (!result.success) throw result;
        const data = movementRows(result);
        const titles = {
            movement:'📋 حركات المخزون',
            stock_detail:'📦 تفاصيل المخزون',
            movements_summary:'📈 ملخص الحركات',
            profit_product:'💰 الربح حسب المنتج',
            profit_supplier:'🏭 الربح حسب المورد',
            profit_customer:'👥 الربح حسب العميل',
            below_min:'📉 المنتجات تحت الحد الأدنى',
            movements_detailed:'📋 حركات مفصلة'
        };
        document.getElementById('report-title').textContent = `${titles[type] || '📊 التقرير'} — ${stockType === 'fuel' ? 'الوقود' : stockType === 'products' ? 'المنتجات' : 'الكل'}`;
        document.getElementById('report-date-range').textContent = `📅 الفترة: ${from || 'البداية'} إلى ${to || 'النهاية'} — ${data.length} سجل`;

        // تجميع حقيقي من البيانات الخام لتقارير الربح والملخصات
        const aggregated = aggregateReportData(type, data);
        updateReportTable(type, aggregated, stockType);
    } catch (e) {
        showToast(e.error || e.message || 'خطأ في توليد التقرير من SQLite', 'error');
    } finally { showLoading(false); }
}

// تجميع تقارير الربح والملخصات من البيانات الخام
function aggregateReportData(reportType, data) {
    if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length === 0) return data;

    const classify = (mt) => {
        const t = String(mt || '').toLowerCase();
        if (['out','damage','return_supplier','sale','waste','fuel_sale'].includes(t)) return 'out';
        if (['in','return','return_customer','purchase','receive','refill'].includes(t)) return 'in';
        return 'other';
    };

    if (reportType === 'profit_product') {
        const map = new Map();
        data.forEach(row => {
            const itemName = row.item_name || row.product_name_ar || row.product_name || row.fuel_name_ar || row.fuel_name || 'غير محدد';
            const key = String(row.stock_type || 'product') + '::' + itemName;
            const b = map.get(key) || {
                item_name: itemName,
                stock_type: row.stock_type || 'product',
                total_sales: 0,
                total_cost: 0,
                movement_count: 0
            };
            const qty = Math.abs(toNumber(row.quantity ?? row.quantity_change ?? row.qty));
            const totalCost = Math.abs(toNumber(row.total_cost ?? row.total_value));
            const unitCost = toNumber(row.unit_cost ?? row.unit_price);
            const kind = classify(row.movement_type || row.movement_subtype);
            b.movement_count++;
            if (kind === 'out') {
                b.total_sales += totalCost > 0 ? totalCost : (qty * unitCost);
            } else if (kind === 'in') {
                b.total_cost += totalCost > 0 ? totalCost : (qty * unitCost);
            }
            map.set(key, b);
        });
        return Array.from(map.values()).map(b => ({ ...b, profit: b.total_sales - b.total_cost }));
    }

    if (reportType === 'profit_customer' || reportType === 'profit_supplier') {
        const map = new Map();
        data.forEach(row => {
            const key = row.party_name || row.supplier_name || row.customer_name || 'غير محدد';
            const b = map.get(key) || {
                item_name: key,
                party_name: key,
                stock_type: row.stock_type || 'product',
                total_sales: 0,
                total_cost: 0,
                movement_count: 0
            };
            const qty = Math.abs(toNumber(row.quantity ?? row.quantity_change ?? row.qty));
            const totalCost = Math.abs(toNumber(row.total_cost ?? row.total_value));
            const unitCost = toNumber(row.unit_cost ?? row.unit_price);
            const kind = classify(row.movement_type || row.movement_subtype);
            b.movement_count++;
            if (kind === 'out') b.total_sales += totalCost > 0 ? totalCost : (qty * unitCost);
            else if (kind === 'in') b.total_cost += totalCost > 0 ? totalCost : (qty * unitCost);
            map.set(key, b);
        });
        return Array.from(map.values()).map(b => ({ ...b, profit: b.total_sales - b.total_cost }));
    }

    if (reportType === 'movements_summary') {
        const map = new Map();
        data.forEach(row => {
            const mt = row.movement_type || row.movement_subtype || 'unknown';
            const itemName = row.item_name || row.product_name_ar || row.product_name || row.fuel_name_ar || row.fuel_name || 'غير محدد';
            const key = String(row.stock_type || 'product') + '::' + mt + '::' + itemName;
            const b = map.get(key) || {
                item_name: itemName,
                stock_type: row.stock_type || 'product',
                movement_type: mt,
                quantity: 0,
                total_cost: 0,
                movement_count: 0
            };
            b.quantity += Math.abs(toNumber(row.quantity ?? row.quantity_change));
            b.total_cost += Math.abs(toNumber(row.total_cost ?? row.total_value));
            b.movement_count++;
            map.set(key, b);
        });
        return Array.from(map.values());
    }

    return data;
}

function updateReportTable(reportType, data, stockType = 'all') {
    const head = document.getElementById('report-table-head');
    const body = document.getElementById('report-table-body');
    if (!head || !body) return;
    head.innerHTML = '';
    body.innerHTML = '';

    const headers = (reportType === 'movement' || reportType === 'movements_detailed')
        ? ['التاريخ','نوع المخزون','الصنف / الوقود','نوع الحركة','الكمية','سعر الوحدة/اللتر','القيمة','المستودع/الخزان','المورد/العميل','المرجع','الحالة']
        : reportType === 'stock_detail'
        ? ['الصنف','نوع المخزون','المستودع/الخزان','الرصيد','متوسط التكلفة/السعر','إجمالي القيمة','آخر حركة']
        : reportType === 'movements_summary'
        ? ['نوع المخزون','نوع الحركة','الصنف / الوقود','الكمية','القيمة','عدد الحركات']
        : reportType === 'below_min'
        ? ['الصنف','نوع المخزون','الرصيد','الحد الأدنى','العجز','القيمة']
        : ['الصنف / الطرف','نوع المخزون','المبيعات','التكلفة','الربح','عدد الحركات'];

    head.innerHTML = '<tr>' + headers.map(h => '<th>' + h + '</th>').join('') + '</tr>';

    if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length === 0) {
        body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="' + headers.length + '" class="text-center">❌ لا توجد بيانات فعلية ضمن نطاق التقرير المحدد</td></tr>';
        return;
    }

    const stockLabel = item => (String(item.stock_type || '').toLowerCase() === 'fuel' || item.item_type === 'fuel') ? '⛽ الوقود' : '🛒 المنتجات';
    const itemLabel = item => item.item_name || item.product_name_ar || item.product_name || item.fuel_name_ar || item.fuel_name || item.name || '---';
    const locationLabel = item => item.location_name || item.warehouse_name || item.tank_name || item.storage_name || '---';
    const partyLabel = item => item.party_name || item.supplier_name || item.customer_name || item.counterparty_name || '---';
    const qty = item => toNumber(item.quantity ?? item.quantity_change ?? item.qty);
    const price = item => toNumber(item.unit_cost ?? item.unit_price ?? item.purchase_price ?? item.price);
    const value = item => toNumber(item.total_cost ?? item.total_value ?? item.value ?? (qty(item) * price(item)));

    data.forEach(item => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        const movement = item.movement_type || item.movement_subtype || '';
        if (reportType === 'movement' || reportType === 'movements_detailed') {
            tr.innerHTML =
                '<td class="numeric">' + escapeHtml(String(item.movement_date || item.created_at || '---').slice(0,19).replace('T',' ')) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + stockLabel(item) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(itemLabel(item)) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + getMovementTypeLabel(movement, item.movement_subtype) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + qty(item).toLocaleString('en', {maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + price(item).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + value(item).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(locationLabel(item)) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(partyLabel(item)) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(item.reference_code || item.reference || '---') + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(item.status || '---') + '</td>';
        } else if (reportType === 'stock_detail') {
            tr.innerHTML =
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(itemLabel(item)) + '</td><td>' + stockLabel(item) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(locationLabel(item)) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + qty(item).toLocaleString('en', {maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + price(item).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + value(item).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + escapeHtml(String(item.last_movement_date || item.last_count_date || item.movement_date || '---').slice(0,10)) + '</td>';
        } else if (reportType === 'movements_summary') {
            tr.innerHTML =
                '<td>' + stockLabel(item) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + getMovementTypeLabel(item.movement_type, item.movement_subtype) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(itemLabel(item)) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + qty(item).toLocaleString('en', {maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + value(item).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + toNumber(item.movement_count || item.count).toLocaleString('en') + '</td>';
        } else if (reportType === 'below_min') {
            const minimum = toNumber(item.minimum_stock ?? item.min_stock_level ?? item.minimum_quantity);
            const available = qty(item);
            tr.innerHTML =
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(itemLabel(item)) + '</td><td>' + stockLabel(item) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + available.toLocaleString('en', {maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + minimum.toLocaleString('en', {maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + Math.max(0, minimum - available).toLocaleString('en', {maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + value(item).toFixed(2) + '</td>';
        } else {
            const profit = toNumber(item.profit);
            const profitColor = profit >= 0 ? 'var(--success-color)' : 'var(--danger-color)';
            tr.innerHTML =
                '<td>' + escapeHtml(itemLabel(item) || partyLabel(item)) + '</td>' +
                '<td>' + stockLabel(item) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + toNumber(item.total_sales ?? item.sales).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + toNumber(item.total_cost ?? item.cost).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric" style="color:' + profitColor + ';">' + profit.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="numeric">' + toNumber(item.movement_count ?? item.count).toLocaleString('en') + '</td>';
        }
        body.appendChild(tr);
    });
}

async function exportReport() {
    const type = document.getElementById('report-type').value;
    if (type !== 'movement') {
        showToast('التصدير المتكامل متاح لتقرير حركات المخزون.', 'warning');
        return;
    }
    showLoading(true);
    try {
        const result = await apiCall('exportInventoryReport', {
            report_type: 'movement',
            stock_type: document.getElementById('report-stock-type').value,
            start_date: document.getElementById('report-from').value,
            end_date: document.getElementById('report-to').value
        });
        if (!result.success) throw result;
        showToast(result.message || 'تم إنشاء ملف CSV الحقيقي وفتحه', 'success');
    } catch (e) {
        showToast(e.error || 'فشل إنشاء ملف التصدير الحقيقي', 'error');
    } finally { showLoading(false); }
}

// ============================================================
// 12. التحليلات
// ============================================================
async function loadAnalytics() {
    try {
        const stockType = document.getElementById('analytics-stock-type')?.value || 'products';
        const result = await apiCall('getMovementStats', { stock_type: stockType });
        if (result.success) {
            const a = bridgeDataObject(result);
            const totalValueEl = document.getElementById('analytics-total-value');
            const totalProductsEl = document.getElementById('analytics-total-products');
            const totalWarehousesEl = document.getElementById('analytics-total-warehouses');
            const belowMinEl = document.getElementById('analytics-below-min');
            if (totalValueEl) totalValueEl.textContent = Number(a.inventory_value ?? 0).toLocaleString('en',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2});
            if (totalProductsEl) totalProductsEl.textContent = Number(a.total_movements ?? 0).toLocaleString('en');
            if (totalWarehousesEl) totalWarehousesEl.textContent = Number(a.inbound_quantity ?? 0).toLocaleString('en',{maximumFractionDigits:3});
            if (belowMinEl) belowMinEl.textContent = Number(a.outbound_quantity ?? 0).toLocaleString('en',{maximumFractionDigits:3});
        }
    } catch (e) { console.error('Error loading analytics:', e); }
}

// توليد مفتاح اليوم بالتوقيت المحلي (لمطابقة movement_date المحلي من SQLite)
function localDateKey(d) {
    const year = d.getFullYear();
    const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

async function loadProductAnalytics() {
    const stockType = document.getElementById('analytics-stock-type').value;
    const days = Number(document.getElementById('analytics-days').value) || 30;
    showLoading(true);
    try {
        const endDate = new Date();
        const startDate = new Date(endDate.getTime() - (days - 1) * 86400000);
        const start = localDateKey(startDate);
        const end = localDateKey(endDate);
        const itemId = stockType === 'fuel'
            ? Number(document.getElementById('analytics-fuel').value || 0)
            : Number(document.getElementById('analytics-product').value || 0);
        if (!itemId) {
            showToast(stockType === 'fuel' ? 'يرجى اختيار نوع الوقود أولاً' : 'يرجى اختيار المنتج أولاً', 'warning');
            showLoading(false);
            return;
        }

        const result = await apiCall('getMovements', {
            stock_type: stockType,
            item_id: itemId,
            product_id: stockType === 'products' ? itemId : 0,
            fuel_type_id: stockType === 'fuel' ? itemId : 0,
            start_date: start,
            end_date: end,
            limit: 5000,
            page: 1
        });
        if (!result.success) throw result;
        const rows = movementRows(result);

        const byDay = new Map();
        rows.forEach(row => {
            // مفتاح اليوم مبني على التوقيت المحلي — مطابق لصيغة movement_date المحلية من SQLite
            const rawDate = String(row.movement_date || row.created_at || '').slice(0, 10);
            if (!rawDate || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(rawDate)) return;
            const bucket = byDay.get(rawDate) || { count: 0, inbound: 0, outbound: 0, value: 0 };
            const q = Math.abs(toNumber(row.quantity ?? row.quantity_change ?? row.qty));
            const t = String(row.movement_type || row.movement_subtype || '').toLowerCase();
            bucket.count += 1;
            bucket.value += Math.abs(toNumber(row.total_cost ?? row.total_value ?? row.value ?? (q * toNumber(row.unit_cost ?? row.unit_price))));
            if (['in','return','return_customer','purchase','receive','refill'].includes(t)) bucket.inbound += q;
            else if (['out','damage','waste','return_supplier','sale','fuel_sale'].includes(t)) bucket.outbound += q;
            byDay.set(rawDate, bucket);
        });

        const labels = [];
        const counts = [];
        const inbound = [];
        const outbound = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < days; i++) {
            const d = new Date(startDate.getTime() + i * 86400000);
            const key = localDateKey(d);
            const b = byDay.get(key) || { count: 0, inbound: 0, outbound: 0 };
            labels.push(key);
            counts.push(b.count);
            inbound.push(b.inbound);
            outbound.push(b.outbound);
        }

        document.getElementById('product-analytics-range').textContent =
            (stockType === 'fuel'
                ? '⛽ ' + (document.getElementById('analytics-fuel-name').value || 'الوقود')
                : '🛒 ' + (document.getElementById('analytics-product-name').value || 'المنتج'))
            + ' — ' + days + ' يوم';

        renderChart(labels, counts, inbound, outbound);
        renderAnalyticsTable(labels, counts, inbound, outbound);
    } catch (e) {
        showToast(e.error || e.message || 'لا توجد بيانات تحليل حقيقية للفترة المختارة', 'error');
        renderChart([], [], [], []);
        renderAnalyticsTable([], [], [], []);
    } finally {
        showLoading(false);
    }
}

function renderAnalyticsTable(labels, counts, inbound, outbound) {
    const body = document.getElementById('analytics-table-body');
    if (!body) return;
    body.innerHTML = '';
    if (!labels.length) {
        body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center">❌ لا توجد بيانات تحليل</td></tr>';
        return;
    }
    labels.forEach((day, i) => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = '<td class="numeric">' + day + '</td>' +
            '<td class="numeric">' + toNumber(counts[i]).toLocaleString('en') + '</td>' +
            '<td class="numeric">' + toNumber(inbound[i]).toLocaleString('en',{maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="numeric">' + toNumber(outbound[i]).toLocaleString('en',{maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="numeric">' + (toNumber(inbound[i]) - toNumber(outbound[i])).toLocaleString('en',{maximumFractionDigits:3}) + '</td>';
        body.appendChild(tr);
    });
}

function renderChart(labels, values, inbound = [], outbound = []) {
    const container = document.getElementById('product-analytics-chart');
    if (!container) return;
    if (chartInstance) {
        try { chartInstance.destroy(); } catch (_) {}
        chartInstance = null;
    }
    if (!labels.length) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center" style="padding:20px;">لا توجد بيانات حركة للفترة المختارة</div>';
        return;
    }
    container.innerHTML = '<canvas id="productChart"></canvas>';
    if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center" style="padding:20px;">مكتبة الرسم البياني غير متوفرة</div>';
        return;
    }
    const datasets = (inbound.some(v => v > 0) || outbound.some(v => v > 0))
        ? [
            { label: 'دخول / إرجاع', data: inbound, borderColor: '#10b981', backgroundColor: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.1)', tension: 0.3, fill: true },
            { label: 'خروج / تلف', data: outbound, borderColor: '#ef4444', backgroundColor: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.08)', tension: 0.3, fill: true }
        ]
        : [{ label: 'عدد الحركات', data: values, borderColor: '#2563eb', backgroundColor: 'rgba(37,99,235,0.1)', tension: 0.3, fill: true }];
    chartInstance = new Chart(document.getElementById('productChart').getContext('2d'), {
        type: 'line',
        data: { labels, datasets },
        options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true } } }
    });
}

// ============================================================
// 13. التهيئة الأولية
// ============================================================
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    restoreTheme();
    document.getElementById('viewModal')?.addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function() {
        inventoryMovementDetailsRequestVersion += 1;
        cancelPendingInventoryMovementDetails();
    });
    onMovementStockTypeChanged();
    toggleAnalyticsSelector();

    const now = new Date();
    const localISO = now.getFullYear() + '-' + String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') + '-' + String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0') + 'T' + String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0') + ':' + String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
    const dateEl = document.getElementById('movement-date');
    if (dateEl) dateEl.value = localISO;

    const today = localDateKey(now);
    const weekAgoDate = new Date(Date.now() - 7*24*60*60*1000);
    const weekAgo = localDateKey(weekAgoDate);
    const reportFrom = document.getElementById('report-from');
    const reportTo = document.getElementById('report-to');
    if (reportFrom) reportFrom.value = weekAgo;
    if (reportTo) reportTo.value = today;

    loadInitialData().then(() => {
        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const productId = params.get('product_id');
        const mode = params.get('mode');
        if (productId && mode === 'in') {
            showCreateModal().then(() => {
                const mt = document.getElementById('movement-type');
                if (mt) mt.value = 'in';
                const ps = document.getElementById('product-select');
                if (ps) ps.value = productId;
                const pd = document.getElementById('product-select-display');
                const p = allProducts.find(x => String(x.product_id || x.id) === String(productId));
                if (pd && p) pd.value = p.product_name_ar || p.product_name || p.name || '';
                toggleMovementFields();
                loadProductInfo();
            });
        }
    });
});
